金融市場投資週報                 2026 年 10 月 19 日
上週重點訊息
◼ 美國 9 月通膨升溫，Fed 年底前再升息機率仍高： 美國 9 月 CPI 年增 3.7%，高於 8 月的
3.4%；核心 CPI 年增率亦由 2.4%升至 2.6%，顯示通膨壓力不只來自能源，核心價格亦有回
升。隔日的 PPI 年增率則由 5.6%升至 6.3%，反映上游成本壓力仍強。通膨重新升溫，使 Fed
即使面對就業降溫，也難以快速轉向寬鬆，市場對年底前再度升息的預期仍高。
◼ 台積電財報創新高，AI 需求強勁但市場轉向檢視融資風險：台積電 10/15 公布其第三季淨利

達 7,631 億元、年增 69%，連續第六季刷新紀錄，第三季美元營收 467.2 億美元，並預估第
四季營收將升至 523～543 億美元；公司同時將 2026 全年營收成長預估提高至約 44%，反映
AI 晶片與先進製程需求仍極為強勁。然而，高利率環境使市場開始檢視 AI 資料中心業者的槓
桿與融資能力，CoreWeave 約 500 億美元債務引發信用疑慮，引發市場波動。
◼ 中東衝突再度推高能源風險，油價高檔加劇全球通膨壓力：胡塞武裝上週攻擊沙烏地阿美設施，
伊朗亦表示採取新的海上攻擊策略，使荷莫茲海峽與波斯灣航運風險再度升高。10/16 布蘭特
原油與西德洲原油期貨雙雙收在近三週高點。
市場展望
◼ 科技股中期趨勢料將不變，但短線波動勢將加劇：近期市場同時面臨通膨升溫、AI 資本支出疑
慮與重要科技財報公佈在即等不確定因素，短線風險偏好可能受到壓抑。在利率維持高檔及市
場評價偏高的環境下，市場短線料將隨財報結果與市場預期而出現較大波動。然而，上週台積
電與 ASML 財報結果整體優於預期，可見 AI 相關企業營運與資本支出尚未出現全面轉弱跡象，

科技產業基本面仍具一定支撐，惟後續宜更加重視企業獲利、現金流及資本支出變動及科技子
產業的表現分化現象。
◼ 中天期美債與投資級債債券兼顧息收與利率風險：美國經濟仍具韌性，加上政府與企業發債供
給維持高檔，可能使市場利率持續處於較高水準。雖然投資級債信用利差位於相對低檔，但企
業獲利、現金流及償債能力仍具支撐，加上較高的美國公債基準利率，使投資級債的利息收益
仍具吸引力。相較之下，短天期債券面臨未來利率下降後的再投資風險，長天期債券則較容易
受到公債供給增加、期限溢酬及利率波動影響，因此中天期美國公債與投資債券較能兼顧息收
與利率風險。

資料中心監管疑慮衝擊 AI 供應鏈，美股全面重挫
2026 年 11 月 06 日
一、 市場動態
1. 資料中心監管疑慮引爆 AI 供應鏈賣壓，美股選後漲勢一日反轉：民主黨取得參、眾兩
院多數，美國進入分裂政府格局。S&P 500 於 11 月 4 日先漲 1.58%，11 月 5 日卻重
挫 4.36%，創 2025 年 4 月以來最大單日跌幅；Nasdaq 跌 5.52%，費半大跌 12.25%，
十一大產業全數收黑。主因有三：一是資料中心開發成為州級政策議題，德州州長當
選人主張暫停新建，全美新增容量約三成可能受影響；二是高通與 Arm 財報不如預

期；三是建設遞延將推高依賴外部融資業者的持有成本。費半重挫前已自 6 月高點回
落近三成，政策不確定性進一步放大跌幅（詳見表一）。
表一：美股主要指數表現（2026/11/05 收盤）
主要指數 收盤點位 單日漲跌幅
S&P 500 指數 7,353.73 −4.36%
Nasdaq 綜合指數 24,315.00 −5.52%
費城半導體指數 9,011.14 −12.25%
資料來源：Reuters、Nasdaq、元大銀行整理
2. 波動率指數急升，公債殖利率全面走低，油價反彈：11 月 5 日避險情緒升溫，VIX 盤
中升至 28.09（前日 16.61）。資金湧入美債，2 年、5 年、10 年及 30 年期殖利率分
別降至 4.46%、4.59%、4.84%及 5.25%，呈牛市陡峭化；12 月升息一碼機率由約
83%降至約 65%。布蘭特原油反彈 3.88%至每桶 98.29 美元，通膨前景仍具不確定性
（詳見表二）。
表二：美債殖利率與避險資產變化（2026/11/05）

二、 下跌原因
1. 民主黨橫掃國會及州長選舉，資料中心開發成為州級政策議題：德州州長當選人 Gina
Hinojosa 主張暫停新建資料中心，俄亥俄州與喬治亞州當選人也提出限制。地方政府

不另行通知。
可透過電價分攤、建設許可及稅務優惠影響新案進度，UBS 估計 2028 年全美新增資
料中心容量約三成可能受影響。國會對科技巨頭與資料中心用電的監督亦可能增強，
半導體、電力設備及資料中心類股成為賣壓重心。
2. 高通手機晶片業績低於預期，加重半導體類股賣壓：高通財年第四季調整後 EPS 年減
28%至 2.17 美元，手機晶片營收年減 26%至 51.37 億美元，低於預期，主因蘋果相
關收入減少；汽車與物聯網營收雖年增 36%，仍難抵銷缺口，股價隔日收跌 9.5%，
進一步放大半導體跌幅。
3. 融資成本與投資回收壓力加重，依賴外部融資業者首當其衝：資料中心資本支出多發

生於上線前，若電力、許可或工程延後，收入認列隨之推遲，利息與持有成本卻須持
續負擔。儘管 12 月升息機率降至約 65%，借款成本仍高，依賴外部融資或單一客戶
合約的 AI 基礎建設業者將面臨回報壓縮與再融資風險。
4. 科技股在本次重挫前已明顯修正，評價落至極低水準：費半自 6 月 22 日高點 14,634.72
點已回落逾 30%，Nasdaq 亦自 10 月 6 日高點 27,599.89 點回落逾 10%。市場質疑
AI 投資效益，降低對高評價企業的容忍度，政策不確定性因而放大修正。近期跌幅較
屬估值下調而非基本面惡化，若晶片訂單與獲利預估維持穩定，市場有望回溫。
表三：11/5 主要科技股及類股表現
標的 單日漲跌幅 重點說明
費城半導體指數 −12.25% 選後跌幅最深，AI 供應鏈評價重估
高通（Qualcomm） −9.5% 手機晶片營收年減 26%，低於預期
軟體類股 ETF（IGV） −4.48% 跌幅明顯小於半導體
資料來源：Reuters、Bloomberg、元大銀行整理

三、 市場看法
1. 短期評價修正而非長期趨勢反轉，AI 資本循環仍處擴張階段：我們視本波為多頭格局
中的健康修正。修正反映股東對龐大資本承諾要求更高風險補償，而非 AI 變現失速：
Google Cloud 第三季營收年增 103%、營業利益年增 209%，四大雲端業者資本支出
預估上修至約 1.16 兆美元，顯示企業看見更大市場而主動加碼（詳見表四）。此外，
政策衝擊集中於州與地方，聯邦仍支持資料中心擴建，選舉結果本質是要求用電成本
內部化，而非否定 AI 投資。
表四：AI 基本面持續擴張──關鍵指標一覽
指標 最新數據 解讀
Google Cloud 第三季營收 年增 103% AI 需求快速轉化為收入
Google Cloud 第三季營業利益 年增 209% 獲利能力未因擴張而惡化
四大雲端業者資本支出預估 約 8,732 億→1.16 兆美元 企業主動加碼，投資循環仍擴張
資料來源：元大投顧、Bloomberg；元大銀行整理

不另行通知。 
2. 留意五大反彈訊號，利空逐步消化後市場可望回穩：（一）州級電價與併網制度若於
2027 年上半年建立，延後專案可望下半年重新加速，眾議院以 417 比 3 通過
《Ratepayer Protection Act》亦顯示跨黨派共識；（ 二）雲端業者資本支出指引未下
修、未見取消訂單；（ 三）11 月 10 日 CPI 及 13 日 PPI 物價月增率回落；（ 四）財政
部維持附息債標售規模，資金回流市場；（ 五）VIX 回落且投資等級債利差穩定。訊號
出現後，市場有望回歸基本面。 
3. 政策先影響建設速度，AI 需求仍須以訂單驗證：州級限制首先改變的是資料中心選址
與交付時程，而非 AI 需求本身。雲端客戶若維持支出計畫，專案可調整順序或轉往他
州，惟設備交付遞延將壓抑供應商近季營收；聯邦立法限制則須經國會及總統否決權，
短期落地難度高。若主要客戶訂單穩定，本次修正較屬建設時程遞延的風險重定價。 
4. 新增算力面臨遞延，既有算力相對有利：高盛估計可能收緊開發限制的州約占美國規
劃中資料中心容量的 45%，其中德州約占 25%。新案放慢下，大型雲端業者既有算力
可望受惠於利用率與議價能力提升，資本支出延後亦減輕現金流壓力；仍待併網的專
案及高槓桿業者則面臨回收遞延，產業鏈分化將擴大。 
5. 歷史經驗顯示選後股市多正向，後續表現仍宜留意基本面變化：彭博統計 1950 年以
來，美國期中選舉後 12 個月 S&P 500 平均報酬約 15%，19 次全數收正，最高為 1954
年的逾 30%，最弱的 1986 年亦小幅上漲。府會分治抑制政策大幅轉向為選後行情主
要支撐，惟後續表現仍須觀察基本面(詳見下圖)。

6. 企業獲利仍強勁成長，本次修正以估值與風險溢價重估為主：彭博 11 月 4 日統計，
S&P 500 已公布財報公司中 79.85%優於預期，資訊科技更達 92.45%，第三季 EPS
成長率分別為 29.0%及 67.8%（詳見表五）。 財報為美股提供中期支撐，短期評價則
取決於 2027 年獲利預估能否承受建設遞延與融資成本壓力。 
表五：S&P 500 第三季財報統計（截至 2026/11/04） 
類別 已公布家數 優於預期家數 優於預期比率 第三季 EPS 成長率 
S&P 500 397 317 79.85% 29.0%

7. 最新經濟數據仍具韌性，聯準會利率路徑仍具變數：初領失業救濟金降至 18.9 萬人，
第三季勞動生產力季增年率 3.6%、單位勞動成本季減 0.7%，經濟仍具韌性。惟油價
逼近每桶 100 美元，若通膨高於預期，升息定價恐再升溫，壓抑科技股估值與長債價
格。
8. 信用市場是判斷風險是否由股權評價擴散至金融條件的重要指標：若 AI 企業持續擴大
資本支出與外部融資，償債能力將受關注。大型投資等級科技企業現金流穩定，較能

吸收壓力；高槓桿或依賴單一客戶的 AI 基礎建設業者則較易受利差上升衝擊。信用利
差若維持穩定，風險仍以股權評價調整為主；若同步擴大，則須警惕金融條件收緊。
四、 投資建議：
1. 短期修正不改 AI 長期趨勢，切勿恐慌殺低錯殺優質標的：本次重挫源自選後政策不確
定性與評價重新定價，AI 需求與企業獲利尚未反轉，屬多頭中的短期波動。應避免恐
慌出清而錯殺優質標的；已持有者以續抱為主，加碼宜分批逢低，避免高槓桿操作。
2. 以股債平衡穿越短期波動，等待基本面重新主導市場：我們認為，本次修正屬短期政
策衝擊與評價調整，中長期科技股展望仍正面。在政策與通膨明朗前，建議將集中度
過高的科技股部位，適度轉向波動較低的平衡型或多重資產商品，搭配優質債券穩定
收益，並保留股票部位分批布局優質標的，待反彈訊號確認後再提高風險資產比重。
3. 股票部位聚焦優質、評價回落且前景較佳的產業：修正後部分優質企業評價已回到合
理區間，股票建議聚焦：一、握有長期電力合約、已通電園區的大型雲端業者；二、
明年展望佳的 AI 供應鏈，如 ASIC、光通訊、電力設備、半導體設備及資安；三、跌

幅較小的軟體及非科技優質股。高度仰賴新建資料中心與外部融資的業者，宜待環境
明朗再評估。
4. 中天期美國公債及高信評投資等級債仍較具優勢：在美國政策風險升溫及科技股評價
調整期間，美國公債仍具投資組合避險功能，但考量通膨與能源價格的不確定性，現
階段不建議延長存續期間。中天期美國公債及高信評投資等級債在兼顧票息收益、利
率敏感度與資產配置穩定性上仍較具優勢。信用債方面，營運現金流充足、槓桿適中
及再融資需求可控的發行人仍較為穩健，對 AI 相關企業則須進一步評估資本支出、合
約現金流與債務到期結構。

### Page 1

Summary: Merida hosted its 3Q26 investor call on 17 November after market hours, 
following release of its 3Q26 results on 11 November (see our memo). Merida’s gross 
margin came in at 15.2% (vs. 19.8% in 2Q26; 13.7% in 3Q25), lower than our estimate 
(16.2%) and the consensus (17.3%). Factoring in the equity investment loss of 
TWD150m+, its 3Q26 EPS was TWD1.06, lower than our forecast (TWD1.68) and the 
consensus estimate (TWD1.65).

We have a Buy (1) rating with a 12-month TP of TWD96, based on a target PER of 17x 
applied on our 1-year-forward EPS forecast. For more information on this company, please 
refer to our latest flash note, 2Q26 review: mainly dragged by Specialized, on 14 August.

What’s the impact 
• 3Q26 results recap. Merida’s 3Q26 revenue of TWD6,098m (-12.1% QoQ, -15.6% 
YoY) missed our and the Bloomberg consensus estimates by 13.6% and 11.1%, 
respectively, which we attributed to the discount when destocking the older models. Its 
gross margin came in at 15.2% (vs. 19.8% in 2Q26; 13.7% in 3Q25), lower than our 
estimate (16.2%) and the consensus (17.3%), due to less OBM sales, the price discount 
for further inventory digestion and the depreciation of the Euro. Factoring in the equity 
investment loss of TWD150m+, its net profit of TWD176m (-44.8% QoQ, -71.9% YoY) 
missed our and the consensus estimates by 65.0% and 65.6%. Its 3Q26 EPS was 
TWD1.06, lower than our forecast (TWD1.68) and the consensus estimate (TWD1.65).  
• 2026 outlook update. Merida revised down its 2026 full-year shipments to 960k units 
(Taiwan plant 280k, down from previous 340k; China plant 680k, down from previous 
700k), as it continues to manage its existing model shipments and expects new models 
in 2027 to bring stronger demand. The company now sees recovery in Europe and 
China, with inventory digestion coming to an end: 9M26 sales in Europe grew 14% YoY, 
driven by strong demand for road bikes and gravel bikes; 9M 26 sales in China grew by 
1% while shipments grew by 7% (vs. revenue guidance of declining 15-20% at the 
beginning of 2026), driven by low- and mid-end models. 
• SBC expected to be profitable in 4Q26. Due to SBC’s conservative tax assumption 
(over 100% effective tax rate) in 1Q-3Q26, if and when the tax rate realised at year-end 
is determined to be less than currently allocated for, we expect there will be a huge profit 
recognised in 4Q26 to offset the estimated after-tax loss recognised in 1Q-3Q26. Merida 
and SBC will review the tax assumption policy after full-year results are out, as the 
policy has caused a great deal of investor confusion. Including USD15m of tariff refund 
in 3Q26, SBC’s pre-tax profit grew by 42% YoY for 9M26. Merida expects there is 
another USD5m of tariff refund to collect in 4Q26.

What we recommend 
We have a Buy (1) rating with a 12-month TP of TWD96, based on a target PER of 17x 
applied on our 1-year-forward EPS forecast. The stock is currently trading at PERs of 
13.2x/10.6x, based on our 2026/27E EPS, vs. its past-5-year one-year-forward PER 
trading range of 7-33x. Key downside risks: slower-than-expected e-bike penetration in the 
EU and the US, worse-than-expected demand in an inflationary environment and inventory 
digestion.

In the interests of timeliness, this document has not been edited.

ab 17 November 2026
Global Research
ASPEED Technology Inc
Near-term BMC momentum peaking;
downgrade to Neutral
Three reasons for our downgrade to Neutral
We remain constructive about Aspeed’s growth potential and competitive positioning
longer term, but are downgrading the stock from Buy to Neutral on limited upside vs
expectation in 2027-28. Key considerations: 1) Aspeed’s BMC unit growth is highly tied
to overall Cloud AI TAM expansion, which is at rising risk especially for 2028 after the US

mid-terms and constraint of power / datacenter construction; 2) our analysis shows that
Aspeed may have over-shipped vs end market server units in 2026, leading to
uncertainty of order normalization in 2027 if tight supply eases / demand slows; 3) we
see limited shipment upside in next several quarters from high base in Q426.
Possible order moderation in 2027, after some over-build this year
Aspeed's BMC units grew 78% YoY to reach ~34mn in 2026, of which we estimate

9.5mn units for accelerator servers, and 23.5mn units for general purpose servers. Our
analysis indicates that Aspeed's BMC shipment for general purpose server likely exceeds
the end market server units at ~22.7m this year, implying excess inventory build at
hyperscalers which could cap the upside into 2027. We note that healthy shipment ratio
of Aspeed's BMC units vs end market should be ~80-90% considering Aspeed's 80%
market share, with 5-10% upside for switch, power supply etc. The ratio likely rises to

104% in 2026 from 70%+ in 2024-25. See Figure 1-3 for our analysis.   We lower 2027
BMC units from 48.2mn to 42.4mn, assuming the BMC shipment to reach near term
peak of 11-12m/Q in Q426-Q127, and to soften moderately to ~10m/Q in H227. We
still consider robust growth of AI accelerater BMCs at 82% in 2027E vs 76% in 2026E.
Product roadmap reinforces long-term leadership
Longer term, we remain constructive on Aspeed’s strong execution, accelerated product

roadmap, and rising silicon content per server. Aspeed remains highly focused on BMC
development, unlike  many competitors where BMC is one of several priorities. Its
roadmap, including more advanced BMCs such as the 6nm AST2800, enhanced security
features, and adjacent products such as SMC, I/O expanders and FPGA integration,
should reinforce its leading position in the Cloud AI market over the next 3-5 years. See
our view on Marvell’s infrastructure management controller (IMC) introduction.

Valuation: D/G to Neutral from Buy; lower target to NT$10,000 from NT$19,000
We trim 2027/28E EPS by 14%/11%. Our new estimates are 10/12% below VA
consensus. Aspeed's order outlook remains robust for 2027, but we see risk of
moderation in coming quarters. Downgrade to Neutral from Buy. Our new target of NT
$10,000 is based on 30x 2027E PE, as we roll back from 43x 2027-28E PE given the
rising uncertainty. 30x PE is below Aspeed's historical mid-cycle of 36x, but we view

reasonable considering the potential demand slowdown and 30% LT earnings CAGR.

### Page 2

ASPEED Technology Inc UBS Research
Thesis MapUBS Research THESIS MAP a guide to our thinking and what´s where in this report
Q: Can BMC units continue to grow in the next two years after rising 78% in 2026E?
Although we remain constructive about Aspeed's long term execution and technology leadership,
we see a possible order moderation into 2027 after over-build in 2026E. We estimate that BMC
shipment will rise 78% YoY in 2026E and reach around 34m total BMC shipment units with 23.5m

dedicated for general purpose servers—this suggests potential pre-building of BMC inventory by
hyperscalers, as end market general purpose server units are only at 22.7m units in 2026E. In
addition, we are also monitoring the risk of AI data center buildout slowdown in 2028 post the recent
US elections and power constraints. We now forecast BMC shipment growth to moderate in
2027E/28E, with 25%/13% YoY unit shipment growth respectively.
Q: Can Aspeed maintain its dominant 70%-plus market share in server BMCs?

Yes. We expect Aspeed to maintain its market share in the next 3-4 years due to 1) We expect
migration to AST2700 to accelerate in 2027 from a mild 2026 ramp up, with new Intel and AMD CPU
platforms as well as key cloud ASIC projects and Nvidia's new Rubin Ultra AI GPU platform in 2027; 2)
Aspeed has enhanced its BMC offering by embedding flexibility to satisfy customer requirements; 3)
Aspeed is expanding its product portfolio to I/O expanders that integrate functionalities built on

FPGA, RoT security chips, and mini-BMCs (SMCs). 4) Aggressive business strategy to build inventory
for hyperscalers since 2025, which we believe should limit opportunity for competitors.
Q: Can Aspeed maintain overall gross margins at a high-60s level?
Yes. We expect Aspeed's GM to remain in the high-60s over the next few years. Drivers include: 1) the
roll-out of the higher-end AST2700 series, which could solidify Aspeed's competitive position and

help it sustain market share; 2) Aspeed managing back-end logistics for AST2700, reducing reliance
on GUC; and 3) Aspeed raising prices (effective Dec 1) for BMCs to pass through cost inflation.
We are constructive for Aspeed's long-term competitive positioning but are downgrading it to
Neutral. Our key considerations: 1) Aspeed’s BMC unit growth is highly tied to overall Cloud AI TAM
expansion, which is at rising risk especially for 2028 after the US mid-terms and constraint of power /

datacenter construction; 2) our analysis shows that Aspeed may have over-shipped vs end market
server units in 2026, leading to uncertainty of order normalization in 2027 if tight supply eases /
demand slows; 3) we see limited shipment upside in next several quarters from high base in Q426.
1) Our analysis indicates that Aspeed's BMC shipment for general purpose server likely exceeds the
end market server units at ~22.7m this year, implying excess inventory build at hyperscalers which

could cap the upside into 2027. 2) The outcome of recent US mid-terms creates higher uncertainty
for new AI data center build-out in 2028.
Aspeed's share price has rallied ~65% YTD (vs Taipei Exchange Index's 11% gain), but declined 54%
from its peak in Sep. The stock is now trading at 29x/22x 2027/28E PE, de-rated from its peak of
around 63x/48x in Sept, suggesting the market is taking a more conservative stance regarding overall
Cloud AI outlook and Aspeed's growth.

Company Description
ASPEED is the world's largest baseboard management controller (BMC) chipset provider.
ASPEED introduced the first generation of server management processors in 2005. It acquired
Emulex Pilot's remote server management chip business in 2016. Besides server management

chipsets, ASPEED also produces PC/AV extension solutions and image processing system-on-
chips (SoCs). In 2023, server management chipsets accounted for 88% of total sales.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
We derive our price target for Aspeed from PE valuation methodology relative to its historical
trading range.
We believe key upside risks for Aspeed include: 1) stronger-than-expected server demand,
particularly from hyperscalers and from AI servers with less crowding out of traditional

servers; 2) further market share expansion; 3) faster-than-expected server platform upgrades;
4) faster-than-expected progress in expanding the addressable content within servers.
We believe key downside risks for Aspeed include: 1) weaker-than-expected server demand,
particularly from hyperscalers; 2) market share losses or more intense industry competition; 3)
slower-than-expected server platform upgrades; 4) slower-than-expected progress in
expanding the addressable content within servers.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

大量(3167 TT) Buy

CPO 設備將於 3Q27 量產，給 Buy 的投資建議。 
投資建議：大量受惠於 AI 帶動 PCB 製造、半導體檢測需求，
獲利大幅成長，CPO 設備將於 3Q27 量產，給 Buy 的投資建議。 
大量受惠於 AI 伺服器帶動高階 PCB 鑽孔設備：大量在±2 mil 
PCB 背鑽計量領域的地位依然穩固；大量受惠於 AI 伺服器帶
動高階 PCB 鑽孔設備需求的趨勢，而非單純受惠某一客戶的拉
貨。 
3Q26 毛利率季增約 6pts： 大量 3Q26 營收 36.71 億元，
QoQ+28.40%；稅後純益 8.99 億元，QoQ+65.51%；稅後 EPS 9.85
元。 
3Q26 毛利率季增約 6pts，主要來自產品組合優化，包含高階
CCD 機種及 TM3、 TM4。一般機種毛利率約 20~30%，高階機
種毛利率約 50%， TM3、 TM4 毛利率更高，TM4 於 3Q26 出貨
約 5 台。 
大量的子公司-大量先進智能規劃 IPO：大量先進智能已經於
08/01/2026 獨立，目前大量持股大量先進智能 100%，預計大量
先進智能上櫃後，大量持股大量先進智能仍會超過 60%。  
大量先進智能的核心技術包含了半導體的精密量測
(Metrology)、 AOI 智慧檢測(Inspection)及 (Automation)自動化。
產品應用包含 CoWoS、 SoIC 及面板級封裝(FOPLP)(CoPoS)、
CPO 等。

2 群益投顧 
  ESG：  
E：(Environmental)。大量科技秉持「愛護地球、永續發展」之理念，持續推
動環境管理制度，並透過日常營運管理落實環境保護與污染預防，降低營運
活動對環境造成之影響。大量科技秉持「愛護地球、永續發展」之理念，持
續推動環境管理制度，並透過日常營運管理落實環境保護與污染預防，降低
營運活動對環境造成之影響。大量科技針對廠區內原物料、製程、生產活動
及服務建立環境考量評估機制，評估範圍涵蓋正常、異常及緊急狀況下可能
產生之環境影響，並納入空氣排放、水污染、廢棄物、能源使用、土地污染、
噪音及其他環境衝擊等項目。 
S：(Social)。大量科技重視人才發展與員工照顧，將員工視為公司穩健營運
與永續經營的重要基礎。大量科技秉持以人為本的精神，致力於培育與留任
專業人才，並透過多元招募、薪酬福利、教育訓練、勞資溝通及權益保障等
制度，持續打造友善、平等且具支持性的職場環境。大量科技於人才任用上
遵循相關勞動法令及多元平等原則，重視員工專業能力發展及工作權益保
障，並持續透過教育訓練提升員工技能，強化員工與公司共同成長之基礎。
G：(Governance)。大量科技致力於推動產品朝高規格、高自動化、高智能化
及完全整合化之方向發展，以貼近客戶需求，並與客戶共同開發新產品。此
外，公司持續投入自動化設備之研發，厚植核心技術能力，並持續開發進階
產品，以掌握市場先機。為確保制度有效運作，大量科技於企劃室下設企業
誠信經營小組，由總經理擔任召集人，負責誠信經營政策之推動與執行情形
之監督，並每年向董事會報告執行成果及防範不誠信行為之措施。大量科技
依董事會通過之「誠信經營守則」及相關內部規章，建立對於不誠信行為之
相關防範機制，並透過內部控制、檢舉制度及教育訓練等措施，確保誠信經
營政策之落實。 
公司簡介： 
大量的產主要產品應用於製造、半導體先進製程與先進封裝，以及光電、玻
璃基板與精密材料加工等領域。透過自主研發之控制器、應用軟體、視覺定
位、深度控制、運動控制及量測檢測技術，大量科技依客戶製程需求提供設
備及技術服務。 
PCB 設備：PCB 鑽孔定位、PCB 成型切割、邊緣滾塗、針塗等塗佈製程及
高速型內層銅深度量測機等。 
半導體設備：CMP Pad 即時量測監控設備、3D IC 先進封裝製程晶圓及晶片
量測設備、FOPLP 形貌量測設備、3DIC 先進封裝製程檢測設備、Flip Chip 
製程檢測設備、捲帶包裝晶片本體及外觀缺陷之檢查設備等、封裝製程自動
化設備等。 
大量 PCB 客戶的客戶包含台灣、中國的 PCB 廠；半導體設備客戶包含晶圓
代工廠、OSAT。  
高階背鑽設備的市場，主要同業為德國 Schmoll Maschinen。  
資料來源：大量年報。

PCB 設備包含鑽孔機、成型機和塗膠設備。鑽孔設備也包含量測設備(TM3、
TM4 系列，應用於背鑽檢測)。  
大量有找外包廠商來增加產能： 
考量標準產品與高階產品，大量目前自有的月產能約 400 台，預計到 2027
年每月會達 500 台，大量會再增加外包的產能約 100~150 台。 
大量的子公司-大量先進智能規劃 IPO：  
大量先進智能已經於 08/01/2026 獨立，目前大量持股大量先進智能 100%，
預計大量先進智能未來上櫃後，大量持股大量先進智能仍會超過 60%。  
大量先進智能的核心技術包含了半導體的精密量測(Metrology)、AOI 智慧檢
測 (Inspection)及 (Automation)自動化。產品應用包含 CoWoS、 SoIC 及面板
級封裝(FOPLP)(CoPoS)、 CPO 等。

資料來源：大量法說簡報。 
2027 年 PCB 與半導體設備展望皆正向： 
PCB 設備 2027 年每月都有要交機，交期約 5~6 月；半導體設備 B/B ratio 維
持在 2 以上。 
大量預估 2027 年 CCD 成型機與鑽孔機將維持雙位數成長，TM3 完成階段
性任務後出貨將減少，TM4 出貨量預期會有大幅倍數成長。PCB 在手訂單
有達千台。

8 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】 
  評等 定義 
  強力買進(Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間≥ 35%  
  買進(Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間<35%  
  區間操作(Trading Buy) 5%≤首次評等潛在上漲空間<15%

中立(Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等 
預期近期股價將處於盤整 
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】 
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。  
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。 
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」、「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。 
停止推薦情境： 
1. 達目標價。 
2. 雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。 
3. 推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】 
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

率創歷史新高。Interplex 已完整併表，單月貢獻金額來到 6700

毛利的 Power 產品最為明顯，根據客戶規劃，1.6T AEC 也將逐步

考量貿聯獲利將維持高成長期，唯獨近期市場擔憂資料中心建置速
度恐受高利率環境而放緩，存在一定 AI 伺服器延宕部屬之風險，
因此研究部下修 PER 至 22x，目標價 2900 元，維持買進。 
⚫ 公司基本資料 ⚫ 公司基本資料 
貿聯-KY(3665)成立於 1996 年，為全球連接線大廠，總部位於美
國矽谷，生產據點則廣泛分布在全球 37 地。產品應用包含數據通
訊、工廠自動化、半導體設備、醫療、車用等，2025 年工業佔 35%、
通訊 42%、車用 11%、電器 11%、其他 1%。銷售主要為美國 20%、
中國 20%、德國 16%、馬來西亞 7%、台灣 3%、其他 34%。

Interplex 正式併表，產品組合有望再度優化 
貿聯 10 月營收 118.5 億元，M+26.9%，Y+80.0%，自 9 月起完成
對 Interplex 的收購，10 月已完整貢獻營收，單月貢獻金額高達
6700 萬美金，約 20 億台幣，由於 Interplex 既有客戶與貿聯本身
有所重疊，然而產品項目卻有所不同，預計在透過集團整合能力下
將進一步推動公司在客戶群內滲透率的提升。此外貿聯本身新規格
的 Power 產品也持續提高出貨，研究部預期產品組合有望再度優
化，進一步推升獲利率表現。綜合以上，研究部預估貿聯 26Q4 營
收 349.1 億元，Q+25.3%，Y+76.5%，毛利率 33.84%，稅後淨利
56.7 億元，Q+32.0%，Y+106.8%，EPS 28.39 元；2026 年營收
1069.3 億元，Y+50.1%，毛利率 31.83%，稅後淨利 152.1 億元，
Y+69.0%，EPS 76.23 元。

2027 年 HPC 產品維持價量齊揚，光通訊產品 2028 年有望發酵 
隨著 Vera Rubin 進入放量，Rack Busbar 升級至 5000A，Power 
Whip 規格從 60A 升級至 100A 且用量增加，1.6T AEC 也將逐步
開始貢獻營收，另外 Interplex 的加入所帶來的產品優化和客戶關
係的綁定，研究部認為貿聯 HPC 事業仍將迎來顯著的價量齊揚增
長。光傳輸領域上貿聯也持續耕耘，除了新富生 MPO 相關產品，
OCP 上與 ams Osram 的合作也意味著公司已著手布局下一代
Scale up 的通訊方案，研究部看好 wide-and-slow 架構在未來的導
入機會以及市場，預計 2028 年後的發酵有望帶來營運成長動能。
綜合以上，研究部預估貿聯 2027 年營收 1608.0 億元，Y+50.4%，

結論 
高毛利產品合計來到 59%，此外營運槓桿帶來的效益使本業獲利率創歷
史新高。Interplex 已完整併表，單月貢獻金額來到 6700 萬美金，除了
擴大產品線外，合併後同樣有助於加深既有客戶的合作關係。展望 2027
年，HPC 事業將迎來產品價量齊揚，尤其以高毛利的 Power 產品最為明
顯，根據客戶規劃，1.6T AEC 也將逐步起量，因此可預期 HPC 占比將
會再進一步提升，利於整體營運表現。綜合以上，研究部預估貿聯 2026
年 EPS 76.23 元，2027 年 EPS 131.41 元，目前 PE 為 11.6x，參考歷
史 PE 區間為 10-35x，考量貿聯獲利將維持高成長期，唯獨近期市場擔
憂資料中心建置速度恐受高利率環境而放緩，存在一定 AI 伺服器延宕部
屬之風險，因此研究部下修 PER 至 22x，目標價 2900 元，維持買進。

### Page 1

(005380) 
현대차 
2026.10.30

이재일 CFA 
6183 / lee.jae-il@eugenefn.com

3Q26 Review: 4 분기 실적 반등 기대감 Up  
 투자의견 BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 600,000 원(유지) 
 현재주가 292,500 원(10/29)

 동사의 3분기 실적은 매출액 45.1조원(-3.4%yoy), 영업이익 2.68조원
(+5.5%yoy, OPM 5.9%), 지배이익 1.7조원(-24.6%yoy)을 기록하며 시장 
컨센서스를 상회. 도매 판매 감소, 원가 부담 증가 영향 있었으나 판매보증
충당금 축소와 원가 절감 활동을 통해 수익성 방어 
 실적 증감 요인: 영업이익 증감 요인은 물량 -1.11조원, 믹스/인센티브 -
0.57조원, 금융 -0.11조원, 환율 +1.28조원, 기타 +0.64조원 등. 원자재 
가격 상승으로 5천억원의 비용 증가 요인 있었으나 위기 대응 원가 절감 
활동으로 대부분 상쇄할 수 있었음. 전분기 대비 기말환율 급락함에 따라 
판매보증충당금 지난 해 1.26조원에서 340억원으로 약 1.23조원 감소. 판
보충 효과는 일회성에 그치겠지만 원가 절감 효과 컸던 점 긍정적  
 금융 부문: 금융부문 매출액은 전년비 2.8% 증가했지만 영업이익은 26% 
급감. 장기 금리 상승에 따른 이자 비용 증가와 기업금융 충당금 적립, 중고
차 관련 비용 확대가 수익성 악화의 주요 원인. 중고차 관련 비용은 EV 리
스 반납 차량의 매각 손실 확대에 기인한 것 
 4분기 전망: 동사는 국내 공장 정상화와 아반떼, 투싼, GV90, GV80 HEV 
등 주요 신차 효과를 통해 4분기 생산 확대와 주요 수출 시장 공급 회복이 
가능할 것으로 전망하였음. 원가 부담은 지속되나 물량 감소에 따른 영향에
서는 벗어날 수 있을 것. 원자재 가격 상승에 대응해 원가절감 프로그램 확
대와 신차 출시를 통한 인센티브 정상화를 통해 수익성 개선 지속할 계획 
 연간 가이던스 하향 조정: 글로벌 도매 판매 목표를 기존 416만대에서 
400만대로 하향 조정. 매출 성장률은 +1~2%, OPM 가이던스는 기존 
6.3~7.3%에서 5.5~6.5%로 하향 조정. 자동차 부문 투자 계획은 17.8조
원에서 16.0조원으로 축소. 핵심 R&D 투자는 유지하되 Capex 집행 시기 
및 우선 순위 조정할 계획 
 4분기 실적 반등 전망: 4분기 주요 신차(투싼, 아반떼, GV80 HEV, GV90) 
출시 본격화로 3분기까지의 부진을 만회할 수 있을 것으로 기대. 원가 부담 
은 비용 절감 활동을 통해 충분히 만회가 가능한 것으로 3분기 실적을 통
해 확인됨. 본격적인 주가 반등을 위해서는 판매 회복 이상의 주가 트리거 
필요할 것으로 보이나, 4분기 반전의 실마리가 나타나고 있다는 점에 주목. 
동사에 대한 투자의견 매수와 목표주가 60만원을 유지함

Compliance Notice 
당사는 자료 작성일 기준으로 지난 3 개월 간 해당종목에 대해서 유가증권 발행에 참여한 적이 없습니다 
당사는 본 자료 발간일을 기준으로 해당종목의 주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다  
당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에도 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

투자기간 및 투자등급/투자의견 비율

### Page 1

2026.10.30   
한화에어로스페이스  
(012450)

3Q26: 그래도 믿는다  Company Brief

3분기 매출 6조 943억원/영업이익 4,520억원 
한화에어로스페이스의 3분기 매출은 6조 943억원/영업이익 4,520억원(OPM 7.4%)로 
컨센서스와 당사 추정치를 모두 대폭 하회하는 실적을 기록하였다. 앞서 실적을 발표한 
한화오션의 실적 쇼크를 감안하더라도, 주력사업인 지상방산 부문의 실적이 매출 1조 
2,455억원/영업이익 217억원(1.7%)로 지난 분기(2조 1,075억원/ 5,330억원/ 25.3%) 
는 물론 전년동기(2조 1,098억원/5,725억원/27.1%)보다도 현저히 감소하였다. 
일회성 요인은 대전사업장 폭발사고에 따른 복구비용 등 300억원, 일부 국내사업 
충당금 160억원 및 환율하락에 따른 효과 등이 있었으나 역시 이를 감안해도 내수와 
수출 모두 실질 이익률은 5%미만으로 추정된다. 지상방산 분기 매출은 1분기(1조 
2,211억)보다 오히려 소폭 증가하였으나 영업이익이 1분기 2,087억원에서 
217억원으로 대폭 감소한 점에 대하여 회사는 프로젝트별 믹스 구성에 따른 낮은 
이익률의 인도분이 반영되었다고 설명하고 있으나, 그럼에도 불구하고 수출과 내수 
이익률 모두 BEP에 가까웠다는 점은 쉽게 이해가 가지는 않는다. 다만 방산기업의 
특성상 대전사업장 사고에 따른 실제 납기 영향 등 회사가 공개할 수 없는 사정이 
있었을 것으로 추측할 뿐이다. 대전사업장은 현재 복구작업이 진행 중이며 27년 
상반기 중에는 가동 정상화할 것으로 예상된다.  
긍정적인 소식도 있다. 폴란드향 천무의 연간 인도대수가 기존 가이던스인 40대에서 
60대로 증가하였으며, 이를 포함하여 3분기에 인도되지 않은 물량의 대부분이 
4분기에 집중될 예정으로, 올해의 4분기 실적 개선폭은 통상적으로 볼 수 있는 
4분기의 계절적 집중도보다 더 커질 것으로 예상한다. 방산기업의 실적은 인도일정에 
따라 크게 변할 수 있는 분기 실적보다 연간 합산이 더 중요하다는 점을 한 번 더 
믿고, 4분기를 기다리는 게 좋겠다.

투자의견 BUY 유지, 목표주가 1,200,000원 하향  
투자의견 BUY를 유지하지만, SOTP기반 목표주가는 기존 1,490,000원에서 
1,200,000원으로 19.5% 하향한다. 비영업가치 중 자회사 한화시스템과 한화오션, 
쎄트렉아이의 지분가치 하락(합산 8.7조원→7.1조원), 그리고 지난 분기 대비 늘어난 
별도 순차입금(3.25조→4.95조원)을 반영하였으며 최근 방산섹터 전반의 밸류 하락을 
감안하여 영업가치 중 지상방산+항공우주의 Target P/E를 기존 35배에서 30배로 
조정하였다. 
3분기는 여러모로 악재가 겹쳤다. 주식시장 전반의 하락에 더해 방산섹터 수주 지연에 
따른 디레이팅, 그리고 납기 지연에 따른 실적 하락 등 나올 수 있는 악재는 다 
나왔다고도 볼 수 있다. 그러나 뒤집어보면 지연중인 수주는 시간의 문제일 뿐 가시화 
가능성이 매우 높으며, 실적 또한 진행률 인식이 아닌 인도기준 인식에 따른 일시적 
현상으로 4분기 이후 복구될 가능성이 크다. 펀더멘털의 유의미한 변화는 없다고 볼 
수 있으며 밸류에이션 매력도가 증가한 현 시점에서 추가 매수를 고려할 때다. 다만, 
대전사업장 사고에 따른 당국의 제재조치가 아직 확정되지 않았음은 잔여 리스크 
요인이다. 동종사의 신규계약 입찰제한 제재조치가 주가에 상당한 영향을 미쳤음을 
고려하면 한화에어로스페이스 또한 제재조치 여부를 지켜본 후 투자할 것을 권한다.

Document 262: AI 投研时代，行情变快了吗？

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI 投研时代，行情变快了吗？.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-20
Pages: 5
Status: ok

### Page 1

天⻛证券通过对⽐本轮科技股调整与历史⽜市⻅顶，结合申万⼆级⾏业数据得出三点结
论：⼀、有业绩⽀撑的周期股逻辑⻅底更快、跌幅更深；⼆、 AI 投研时代⾏情⻅底时间整
体拉⻓；三、⽆业绩⽀撑的纯叙事上涨，下跌共识形成速度加快。
摘要：
核⼼结论
AI 投研时代，⾏情发展的速度是否会受到影响？把⾃六⽉底以来的调整与历史上不同⽜市⻅顶
下跌放在同⼀组价格路径指标下⽐较，综合下跌速度和共识形成的速度，结合本轮科技⾏情既
具备类似  2007-2008 的周期股逻辑也具备类似  2015 年移动互联⽹的产业叙事逻辑，我们认为
本轮调整可以理解为与⾏情结构基本相符。
扩展到更多⾏业以及更普遍的⻅顶回撤，得出以下三点结论：
正⽂摘要
AI 投研时代，⾏情发展的速度是否会受到影响？本⽂通过两步回答这个问题，先把⾃六⽉底以
来的调整与历史上不同⽜市⻅顶下跌放在同⼀组价格路径指标下⽐较，回答 “ 这次是否不⼀样 ” ；
再扩展到历史上多轮周期⾏业与  叙事⽀撑估值的⾏业⻅顶调整，⽐较两种调整之间、 AI 投研时

代与前  AI 投研时代之间的区别。
下跌速度⽽⾔，本轮相较可⽐样本没有额外加速特征。调整前置程度⽽⾔，  A 股可⽐样本中不
少  D60/D90 接近  100% ，或意味着历史上  60 个交易⽇差不多初步⻅⽀撑，这次数字更⼩但
T+N ⽇累积跌幅却没有显著偏离，我们认为可以初步表明本轮⽤ “ 历史相近的速率调整了更久 ” 。
综合下跌速度和共识形成的速度，结合本轮科技⾏情既具备类似  2007-2008 的周期股逻辑也具
备类似  2015 年移动互联⽹的产业叙事逻辑，本轮调整可以理解为与⾏情结构基本相符。
本轮科技⾏情兼具周期股逻辑与产业叙事逻辑，为了讨论这两种逻辑⽀撑的上涨之间、有⽆  AI
投研影响之间，是否存在下跌速率的变化。
本⽂从申万⼆级⾏业符合⻅顶下跌标准的⾏情段中分别： 1 ）取周期股⾏业且有业绩⽀撑的⾏情
段作为周期股逻辑样本， 2 ）取⾮周期⾏业且不存在业绩⽀撑的⾏情段作  为纯叙事拔估值逻辑的
观察样本。得出以下三点结论：
有业绩周期股逻辑相⽐纯叙事拔估值逻辑，⻅底更快、跌幅更深；

AI 投研时代，⾏情⻅顶下跌的⻅底时间或更⻓；
AI 投研时代，没有业绩⽀撑的纯叙事上涨，下跌共识或形成更快。
结论⼀：有业绩周期股逻辑相⽐纯叙事拔估值逻辑，⻅底更快、跌幅更深。
结论⼆： AI 投研时代，⾏情⻅顶下跌的⻅底时间或更⻓。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

AI 投研时代，⾏情变快了吗？
AI 投研时代，⾏情发展的速度是否会受到影响？本⽂通过两步回答这个问题，先把⾃六⽉底以
来的调整与历史上不同⽜市⻅顶下跌放在同⼀组价格路径指标下⽐较，回答 “ 这次是  否不⼀样 ” ；
再扩展到历史上多轮周期⾏业与叙事⽀撑估值的⾏业⻅顶调整，⽐较两种调整之间、 AI 投研时
代与前  AI 投研时代之间的区别。
历史⽜市⻅顶⽐较：本轮调整与⾏情结构基本相符
如何衡量⾏情发展的速度，即所谓 “ 跌的快 ” ？这个问题可以分成，是固定窗⼝跌幅更⼤，  还是最
猛烈的⼏天更陡，还是跌幅前置程度更深。前两者衡量下跌的速度，后者衡量共识形成的速
度。
下跌速度⽽⾔，本轮相较可⽐样本没有额外加速特征。最差  N ⽇跌幅结果显⽰，本轮⾏业和个
股确有剧烈调整段，但历史上也出现过更深的连续窗⼝如  2007-2008 年的煤炭、有⾊，以及
2015 年移动互联⽹⾏业的最差  5/10/20 ⽇回撤整体不弱于本轮； 2000 年前后费城半导体指数
的⻓窗⼝回撤也很深。
⾃⻅顶后  N 个交易⽇的累积跌幅、⾸次触及  10%/20%/30% 所需交易⽇也同样呈现处于历史较

快⽔平，但仍在常规范畴的特征。
调整前置程度⽽⾔，⽐较前  M ⽇的最⼤回撤 / 前  N ⽇的最⼤回撤 (M<N ），衡量是不是共识形成
更快，如  D5/D20 为⻅顶后  5 交易⽇最⼤收盘回撤 / ⻅顶后  20 交易⽇最⼤收盘回撤。 A 股可⽐样
本中不少  D60/D90 接近  100% ，即  90 ⽇内的最⼤回撤⼤多已在前  60 ⽇出现。这或意味着历史
上  60 个交易⽇差不多初步⻅⽀撑，这次数字更⼩但  T+N ⽇累积跌幅却没有显著偏离，我们认
为可以初步表明本轮⽤ “ 历史相近的速率调整了更久 ” 。
综合下跌速度和共识形成的速度，结合本轮科技⾏情既具备类似  2007-2008 的周期股逻辑也具
备类似  2015 年移动互联⽹的产业叙事逻辑，我们认为本轮调整可以理解为与⾏情结构基本相
符。
结论三： AI 投研时代，没有业绩⽀撑的纯叙事上涨，下跌共识或形成更快。

### Page 3

两类⾏业⻅顶回撤⽐较： AI 投研时代⾏情速率有何变化
第⼀部分我们观察到，本轮调整与⾏情结构基本相符，但或存在调整时间更久的特征。
考虑到上⽂所选样本仅为历史上较⼤级别⽜市的⻅顶，要想回答是否速率变快以及确认 “ 调整时
间更久 ” 是否准确，我们认为需要扩展到更多⾏业以及更普遍的⻅顶回撤。
本轮科技⾏情兼具周期股逻辑与产业叙事逻辑，为了讨论这两种逻辑⽀撑的上涨之间、有⽆  AI
投研影响之间，是否存在下跌速率的变化。
本⽂从申万⼆级⾏业符合⻅顶下跌标准的⾏情段中分别： 1 ）取周期股⾏业且有业绩⽀撑的⾏情
段作为周期股逻辑样本， 2 ）取⾮周期⾏业且不存在业绩⽀撑的⾏情段作为纯叙事拔估值逻辑的
观察样本。
定义 “ ⻅顶下跌 ” 的标准为： 1 ）前置上涨：阶段⾼点⾄少⽐此前  252 个交易⽇内的最低收盘价⾼
30% ； 2 ）⻅顶下跌触发：从该阶段⾼点回撤达到  20% ； 3 ）事件结束：从低点反弹  10% 或者当
前距离低点已经过去半年。
定义 “ 有业绩⽀撑 ” 的标准为：⻅顶所在季度前后各两个季度的  5 个季度⾥，⾄少出现连续两个

季度同时满⾜⾏业营收 TTM 同⽐⼤于 0 、⽑利率 TTM 环⽐上升的公司占⽐⼤于 50% 。
⽤  2024/1/1 作为有⽆  AI 投研影响的分界线，并⽤  2025/1/1 做为敏感性分析。
结论⼀：有业绩周期股逻辑相⽐纯叙事拔估值逻辑，⻅底更快、跌幅更深
有业绩周期股逻辑与纯叙事拔估值逻辑横向⽐较，有业绩周期股逻辑的⾏情⻅底更快，跌幅更
深。
有业绩周期股逻辑在  2024 年以前的⻅底所⽤交易⽇中位数为  35 ⽇， 2024 年后  延伸⾄  40
⽇；纯叙事拔估值逻辑  2024 年以前的⻅底所⽤交易⽇中位数为  45 ⽇， 2024 年  后延伸⾄  81
⽇。
周期股⻅顶后第  5/20/30 交易⽇跌幅通常更深， 24 年前 / 后有业绩周期股  逻辑  D5/20/30 最⼤收
盘回撤中位数分别为 -10.7%/-21%/-28.3% 及 -8.8%/-15.6%/-20.9% ；⽽纯叙事拔估值逻辑各个维
度的中位数最⼤收盘回撤均更⼩。
以  2025/1/1 为分界线后，两组中位数同样⽀持有业绩周期股逻辑⻅顶⾄最低点所⽤交易⽇更

短， D5/20/30 最⼤回撤更深的结论。

### Page 4

结论⼆： AI 投研时代，⾏情⻅顶下跌的⻅底时间或更⻓
各个⼝径⽐较下，均显⽰进⼊  24/25 年后，⻅顶下跌的过程被⼀定程度拉⻓。两个截断⼝径的
⽅向相同，⽆论是周期⾏业 / ⾮周期⾏业全体，还是有业绩⽀撑的周期股逻辑与纯叙事拔估值逻
辑样本下，⻅底所需时间⼤多更⻓。
但最⼤跌幅中位数并没有随时间延⻓⽽同步加深， D30 回撤总体相近或更浅，因⽽更准确的描
述是下跌过程拉⻓，不是近期跌得更深或全程更快。
结论三： AI 投研时代，没有业绩⽀撑的纯叙事上涨，下跌共识或形成更快
D5/D20 跌幅⽐衡量的是⻅顶后  5 ⽇与  20 ⽇最深跌幅之⽐，数值越⼤则说明价格的下修在  前  5
个交易⽇更集中，可以作为对共识形成速度的代理变量。
纯叙事拔估值逻辑的  D5/D20 中位数在  24 年截断下从  45.9% 提升到  51.6% ，在  25 年截断下从
45.9% 提升到  60.7% ；没有业绩⽀撑的周期⾏业也有同样规律。对于有业绩周期股逻辑，则不
⽀持此种观察。
注意截断到  25 年后，前置程度提升更⾼，某种程度上说明在  AI 投研更完善的时间，纯叙事上

涨的下跌共识形成更快。

### Page 5

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

Điểm nhấn thị trường 
Chỉ số, nhóm ngành, khối ngoại và phái sinh

Top 5 ngành tăng / giảm  VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tuần qua, -29.18 điểm tương ứng -1.68%, với thanh khoản suy 
giảm. Dầu khí, Bảo hiểm và Dịch vụ phân phối là các nhóm ngành có mức giảm rõ rệt nhất 
trong tuần. Khối ngoại duy trì bán ròng so với xu hướng của tuần trước với giá trị -3,013 tỷ 
đồng, tập trung ở các mã MCH, VIC và FPT; mặt khác, tự doanh đảo chiều mua ròng ở mức 
887 tỷ đồng.

Ở một khía cạnh khác, khối ngoại đảo chiều bán ròng -2,021 HĐTL VN30F1M trong tuần 
qua và đang duy trì vị thế bán lũy kế -4,384 hợp đồng. Tự doanh đảo chiều bán ròng -
4,275 hợp đồng và đang duy trì trạng thái lũy kế bán ròng -7,815 hợp đồng.

TTCK Việt Nam tiếp tục điều chỉnh tuần thứ tư liên tiếp, khi VN-Index lùi về đóng cửa 1.706 
điểm - giảm khoảng 1,7% so với tuần trước và đang dao động quanh vùng hỗ trợ tâm lý 
1.700 điểm, cũng là vùng đáy cũ của tháng 8. Thanh khoản thị trường tiếp tục suy yếu, 
phản ánh tâm lý thận trọng cao độ trong bối cảnh thị trường thiếu vắng thông tin hỗ trợ 
trong nước, trong khi các yếu tố bên ngoài như xung đột tại Iran, và chi phí vốn toàn cầu 
chưa hạ nhiệt tiếp tục đè nặng tâm lý nhà đầu tư. Khối ngoại duy trì chuỗi bán ròng kéo 
dài nhiều phiên liên tiếp, nối dài đà rút vốn đã lên tới xấp xỉ 95 nghìn tỷ đồng từ đầu năm. 
Trong một, hai tuần tới, mùa báo cáo kết quả kinh doanh quý 3 được kỳ vọng là chất xúc 
tác giúp thị trường giao dịch cân bằng trở lại.

Ngưỡng kỹ thuật

VNINDEX tiếp tục hình thành mẫu nến giảm điểm tiêu cực trên đồ thị tuần, đánh dấu tuần 
giảm thứ 4 liên tiếp, cho thấy áp lực phân phối vẫn đang có phần lấn át. Trên khung ngày, 
tuy chỉ số đã có nỗ lực phục hồi trở lại trong 3 phiên đan xen, đặc biệt là phản ứng trong 
phiên cuối tuần khi chạm trendline trung hạn, nhưng việc chỉ có thể hình thành các mẫu 
nến "spinning" hàm ý đà tích lũy chưa thực sự thuyết phục. Trong một vài phiên kế tiếp, 
VNINDEX có thể xuất hiện nhịp hồi kỹ thuật từ vùng hỗ trợ, tương ứng với cạnh dưới tam 
giác, nhưng sau khi chớm phá vùng hỗ trợ quanh 1710 và hiện chỉ còn một số ít các mã giữ 
được xu hướng tăng tính từ đáy tháng 7, xu hướng ngắn hạn đang trở nên khá trung tính.

(Ghi chú: NĐT có thể dựa theo nhận định xu hướng thị trường để tham khảo khuyến nghị 
hành động tương ứng trong bảng phía trên) 
Kháng cự xa: 1780 (+-15) 
Kháng cự gần: 1740 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ gần: 1680 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ xa: 1630 (+-15)

Danh mục đầu tư mẫu

Phương pháp tiếp cận: 
- Danh mục tập hợp các cổ phiếu tiềm năng nhất được KBSV 
lựa chọn và tái cơ cấu theo tháng. 
- Ngưỡng cắt lỗ cần được cân nhắc cho mỗi mã là -15%. 
- Danh mục giả định luôn duy trì tỷ trọng 100% là cổ phiếu với 
trọng số được phân bổ đều giữa các mã.

Lưu ý:  
- NĐT được khuyến nghị mua tại mức giá bằng hoặc thấp hơn 
giá khuyến nghị mua. 
- Lợi nhuận kỳ vọng được tính theo giá khuyến nghị mua. 
 So sánh hiệu suất với VN30 Index

- Nhu cầu thép nội địa sẽ là động lực tăng trưởng chính nhờ (1) Việt Nam đẩy mạnh giải ngân 
đầu tư công và triển khai các dự án hạ tầng trọng điểm, (2) nguồn cung bất động sản dân dụng 
cải thiện, (3) HRC nội địa sẽ thay thế HRC nhập khẩu trong dài hạn.

Document 320: Europe’s Deal Limiting Chinese Auto Imports (2026-11-02)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Europe’s Deal Limiting Chinese Auto Imports (2026-11-02).pdf
Report source: CFR (Follow the Money)
Date: 2026-11-02
Pages: 16
Status: ok

### Page 1

1
EXPERT TAKE FOLLOW THE MONEY
Europe’s Deal Limiting Chinese Auto Imports
Europe’s auto deal with China is a good start. But containing China’s export boom
will require a much broader response.
European Commissioner for Trade and Economic Security Maros Sefcovic speaks at a press briefing at the EU Delegation to
China in Beijing, China October 9, 2026. Florence Lo/Reuters
PUBLISHED
Articles Follow the Money Share
Log In
Regions Topics Research Experts Events The Future of American Strategy

### Page 2

2
November 2, 2026 11:02 a.m.
Brad W. Setser CFR EXPERT
Whitney Shepardson Senior Fellow
The year-over-year increase in Chinese automobile
exports to all markets is currently about 3.5 million
cars.
Over ten years, that is 35 million cars. The global
market excluding China is only around 60 million
cars. Japan raised its auto exports by about 3.5 million
cars in the 1970s—but that was over a decade, not
one year. And right now the U.S. and Indian markets

(around 20 million cars) are effectively closed to
Chinese production. The 2026 jump in exports is an
incredible.
China’s domestic market, though, is down 20 percent
this year—that works out to a decline of about 5
million cars. The Wall Street Journal estimates that
domestic sales will fall to around 19 million cars this
year, less than a third of the production capacity of
China’s auto industry.
So even though China’s exports are increasing at an

unprecedented pace, China’s domestic auto sector
isn’t really booming; production is essentially flat, and
intense domestic competition is destroying the
profitability of firms that don’t export.*

### Page 3

3
Europe’s new auto deal with China should be
understood in this context.
The deal limits China’s exports of hybrids (including
plug-in hybrids) to Europe to around 350,000 cars.
That is above the 300,000 hybrids that China
exported to Europe in 2025, but well below the (gulp)
800,000 annualized pace for hybrids in the last three
months of (European) data. Total imports,
annualized, were running at an incredible 2 million a
year pace thanks to strong battery electric imports in

September.

### Page 4

4
China appears to have concluded that it prefers a
guaranteed market for a limited number of cars (and
more or less guaranteed profits) to the risk of a
prohibitive tariff that would cut off all its exports.
And Europe preferred a negotiated deal to the risk of
retaliation and a slowdown in licensing rare earth and
magnet exports.
The Commission also thinks of this as a bit of a
model—more on that later.
I certainly understand the logic of the deal.

Something needed to be done. China’s export of
hybrids to Europe were up an insane 200 percent
year-over-year in the last 3 months of data, which led
to a 75 percent increase for all cars. One more year of
75 percent growth would bring China’s exports to

### Page 5

5
around 3 million cars; in two years, close to 6 million,
and in three years, 10 million—or most of the market.
But Europe still is a long way from a coherent trade
and industrial policy with respect to autos, let alone a
coherent economic policy towards overall trade with
China.
After this deal, as I understand it:
Hybrid imports are capped at 350,000, down
from a projected 800,000 in 2026—and then
rising to 370,000 and 390,000 in subsequent
years.

Battery electric vehicles face a tariff from 25
percent (Telsa, 10 percent base + 15 percent) to
45 percent (10 percent base plus 35 percent).
BYD’s total tariff is at 27 percent, which isn’t
prohibitive. Even with these tariffs, battery
electric imports will total around 600,000 this
year, up from 500,000 last year.
Diesel and gasoline (ICE) cars face a tariff of
10 percent. Europe is importing around
200,000 of these a year (including a significant

number from Chinese-owned MG).
There are no special tariffs on imported parts or
imported batteries, which matters for the growing
number of Chinese factories in Europe. So long as
tariffs are low and the renminbi is undervalued,
Chinese firms still have an incentive to maximize the

### Page 6

6
Chinese content of their “transplant” factories (ask
Thailand).
There is currently no unified European policy on
inward investment that requires joint ventures or
technology transfer either, only a lot of wishful
thinking.**
Sum it up, and it is a market where Chinese exports
are constrained by a hodge-podge of different trade
defense tools, and there is no standard policy for
ensuring that Chinese assembly operations use
European parts rather than import Chinese kits. The

existing operations, which are just starting to scale up,
don’t draw on a European supply chain. They likely
preserve a few assembly jobs at the expense of the
broader automotive ecosystem.
A coherent European (or national) industrial policy
for the automotive and battery sectors is thus still a
work in progress.
The safeguard on hybrids and the resulting tariff rate
quota only stops the bleeding.
And make no mistake, Europe’s position in the global

automotive sector is slipping. Europe still runs an
overall surplus in auto trade, but that surplus is falling
fast if trade in batteries is added to automotive trade
(there isn’t a split between batteries used for energy

### Page 7

7
storage and batteries used by cars in the EU data
right now, at least not that I have found).
Europe’s broader trade policy to China is similarly a
work in progress.
The surge in imports of hybrid cars was particularly
pronounced, but it is by no means the only such surge.
The balance in industrial machinery has eroded
significantly. Consider the following chart, which
shows imports and exports of machinery, electronics
and vehicles (HS 84, 85, and 87) by weight.

### Page 8

8
The balance in chemicals, measured by weight to
abstract from price changes, shows the same trend.
Europe doesn’t have an across-the-board tool for
applying tariffs against China like the U.S. section
301 statute.

### Page 9

9
But that isn’t necessarily an immediate constraint, as
there are plenty of solid safeguard cases thanks to the
current surge in European imports from China.
But if each safeguard needs to include a negotiated
tariff-rate quota that gives China enough guaranteed
market access (and Chinese firms enough guaranteed
profits) to ensure trade peace, Europe needs to get
moving with negotiation in other sectors.
Chemicals and machinery are more diverse sectors

than cars and thus aren’t as amenable to the same
high-level Š ef č ovi č -Wang negotiations the led to the
most recent deal.
This critique isn’t meant as criticism. Europe needed
to act on cars. Look at a chart of automotive trade in
kilos.

### Page 10

10
It’s just that it also needs to act in other sectors: auto
parts, batteries (though that is complicated),
chemicals, and some categories of industrial
machinery. The scale of China’s export boom and the
fact that in most sectors there are no limits to China’s
ability to scale up is a real problem.***
And Europe cannot wait for China to conclude that
it would rather grow on the basis of internal, rather
than external, demand.
I disagree with the IMF’s former top brass on this. A

China that grows on the back of a 1.5 pp net export
contribution for another couple of years will
deindustrialize much of the world. China’s trading
partners cannot step aside and do nothing until
China figures out how to escape deflation using its
own demand. Xi appears more than happy to
continue with his current policy mix and gamble that

### Page 11

11
the world will continue to absorb China’s ever
growing export surplus.
Pascal Lamy, Nicolas Köhler-Suzuki, and Victor Luiz
do Prado of the Jacques Delors Institute recognize
the need for a more systemic response—as do Jean-
Pierre Landau and Sébastien Jean, who have done an
excellent job explaining how China’s micro- and
macro-industrial policies fit together into a coherent,
self-reinforcing whole.
The proposals in the Lamy et al. paper overlap heavily

with the Commission’s current approach to China.
The authors envision a two-pronged approach: first,
negotiating with China to seek agreement on tariff-
rate quotas that effectively limit the EU’s imports
from China; and second, developing a broad yet
targeted tariff-based instrument to address structural
imbalances should negotiations fail. The latter would
give Europe a credible threat should China refuse to
agree to the proposed safeguards.
My own view: don’t let the perfect be the enemy of

the good.
The EU has shied away from developing its own 301
tool out of concern about preserving a “rules-based
system.” I still think a European 301 would allow a
more fulsome response, but understand that
European isn’t as willing as the United States to use

### Page 12

12
tools that at least appear to be outside those
authorized by the WTO. In reality the EU no longer
really wants to trade with China on the same (MFN)
terms applied to other WTO members. That mirrors
the current view of the United States. But the EU is
willing to go through more procedural hoops to get to
a similar result.****
But I also recognize that, absent policy adjustment in
China—notably a stronger currency that allows for
fair market competition—the scope of safeguards and

managed trade will progressively expand.
* One sign: Stellantis is resuming Jeep production in
China, but primarily for export rather than the
domestic market.
** There is a lot of (in my view) somewhat confused
analysis of Chinese investment that works off the
analogy provided by either foreign investment in
China’s auto sector or Japanese investment in the U.S.
and Europe in the 1980s. These are relevant examples
of course, but asserting that Chinese investment will

result in a similar outcome when the underlying
economics differ is a big logical jump. Japanese firms
had incentives to localize their parts production in the
United States from the then-strong yen as well as
from the risk of protection. Foreign firms operating in
China had to do JVs until recently and had incentives
to localize from China’s significant tariff on auto

### Page 13

13
parts and China’s lower cost structure (in part because
of a weak RMB). Tesla’s Shanghai incentives and tax
deal included strong localization incentives, and there
were additional incentives from Tesla’s desire to
qualify for the lists the governed access to China’s
consumer EV subsidies. That is why something like
90 percent of Tesla parts, including its batteries, are
now made in China. Chinese investors in Europe
have no similar incentives to localize right now, yet

many assume that the results will be the same. That,
to me, is naïve. Since the underlying economics differ
(Europe’s cost basis is much higher than existing
production in China) getting Chinese firms to do
more than final assembly in Europe will take stronger
policy tools than were required to get foreign firms to
localize parts production in China. Europe’s
Industrial Accelerator Act would be a step in the
right direction, but more is likely needed. Thailand

provides a useful cautionary example.
*** China can make over 60 million cars, according to
recent reporting from the Wall Street Journal. The
domestic market this year will be under 20 million
cars (down from something like 24 million cars last
year). Even with exports of 10 million, there is ~30
million cars worth of unused auto production
capacity (roughly double European demand). And
China is still investing at a pace that is consistent
with adding close to 5 million of EV production

capacity a year. Chinese firms, who don’t make any
money on domestic sales, are strongly incentivized to

### Page 14

14
This work represents the views solely of the author(s). The
Council on Foreign Relations is an independent,
nonpartisan membership organization, think tank, and
publisher, and takes no institutional positions on matters
of policy.
From Follow the Money and Greenberg Center for Geoeconomics
export. The world has never encountered such elastic
supply.
**** The more different the legal basis for the
European and U.S. response—and the more policy
differences (TRQs vs. a flat tariff, for example) the

harder it would be to ever develop a harmonized
policy approach. But with President Trump, a
harmonized policy isn’t even a U.S. goal.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS  
OCTOBER CPI PREVIEW
Inflationary pressure fades as we move through year-end
17 November 2026
Key points
• October CPI to rise 0.41% with the annual 
pace lifting to 4.8%yr. The seasonally 
adjusted CPI lifts 0.7%mth, a small step 
up from the 0.6% increase in September.  
• October Trimmed Mean (TM) is set 
to gain 0.42%mth lifting the annual 
pace to 3.7%yr from 3.6%yr. 
• There is a small downside revision 
from our October 19th estimate for 
the December quarter CPI to 1.2%qtr 
while the TM remains 0.9%qtr.  
• The December quarter will represent the 
peak in Australian inflationary pressure. 
Our March quarter CPI remains 0.4%qtr 
and the TM is unchanged at 0.7%qtr. 
• We now see TM inflation dipping below 
3% by Q3 2027 and be well within the 
RBA’s target band by end 2027 at 2.7%yr.
• Demand remains the larger driver of 
inflation, but the recent pick-up in 
trimmed mean inflation appears to 
have been largely supply-driven.

Past performance is not a reliable indicator of future performance. The forecasts given above are predictive in character. Whilst every effort has been taken to ensure that the assumptions on which the forecasts are based are reasonable,

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
Inflation accelerated in September...
The CPI rose 0.8% in the month of September, stronger than 
Westpac’s 0.6% estimate and the market’s expectation of 
0.7%. The annual pace picked up from 4.0%yr to 4.3%yr. The 
seasonally adjusted CPI rose 0.6% in September highlighting 
some positive seasonality in the month. 
Key factors driving the headline monthly result were: auto 
fuels (9.4%mth); domestic holiday travel & accommodation 
(8.0%mth); international holiday travel & accommodation 
(7.2%mth); and property rates & charges (5.5%mth). There 
were modest offsets from: women’s garments (–3.1%mth); 
household textiles (–6.6%mth); and accessories (–4.1%mth).
The more important underlying measure, the Trimmed Mean 
(TM), gained 0.3% in September, spot on both Westpac and 
market expectations with the annual pace lifting from 3.5%yr 
(revised from 3.6%yr) to 3.6%yr. The six-month annualised 
pace is now 4.0%yr the fastest in the short history of this 
series.
Overall, the breadth of inflation moderated for a second 
consecutive month. The share of the basket running above 
3%yr fell from 40% to 35%, while the share below 2%yr 
rose from 27% to 32%. Meanwhile, our replication of the San 
Francisco Fed’s inflation shock momentum index moved into 
positive territory after trending higher since April. Around 
14% of the CPI basket, on an expenditure-weighted basis, 
experienced positive inflation shocks in September, driven 
by holiday travel, fuel, and clothing & footwear. This was 
partly offset by negative shocks across 7% of the basket, 
concentrated in food & non-alcoholic beverages. For full 
analysis of the September CPI, see “ Inflation accelerating in 
the run up to Christmas”.
... but is set to peak in October.
Looking ahead to October, early indicators were mixed. The 
NAB business survey suggested firms continued to absorb 
a meaningful share of higher input costs, with purchase cost 
growth edging up to 2.4%qtr (+0.1ppt from September), while 
retail price growth slowed sharply to 0.2%qtr (–0.8ppts), 
partly reflecting some moderation in labour cost growth 
(see here). Meanwhile, consumers’ year-ahead inflation 
expectations rose to 5.3% in October, with this rate holding 
steady in November. Fuel prices provided a more pronounced 
source of inflationary pressure, with retail petrol and diesel 
prices jumping 8.7% and 5.3%, respectively, in October to 
average $2.40/l and $2.82/l as global oil prices approached a 
local peak.
October to mark the peak in inflation
Justin Smirk, Senior Economist
Neha Sharma, Economist

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
3
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
We expect the October CPI to rise 0.4% (0.41% to 2dp), lifting
annual inflation to 4.8%, which we expect to mark the peak.
On a seasonally adjusted basis, we estimate a stronger 0.7%
increase (0.68% to 2dp). For the monthly trimmed mean, we

expect a 0.4% rise (0.42% to 2dp), nudging the annual pace up
to 3.7%. On a 3m/3m basis, the TM is tracking a 1.0% increase
in October. While conceptually different from the quarterly
trimmed mean, this measure nonetheless provides a useful
guide to near-term underlying inflation momentum. For market
services excluding volatiles & holiday travel, we have pencilled
in a 0.3% rise, lifting annual inflation to 4.0% from 3.8%,
which had marked a ten-month low.

Transport is expected to make the largest contribution
(+0.35ppts), almost entirely reflecting auto fuel (+0.32ppts).
Smaller contributions are expected from clothing & footwear
(+0.06ppts), household goods & services (+0.05ppts), and
meals out & takeaway (+0.04ppts). The main offset comes
from recreation & culture (–0.18ppts), driven entirely by
holiday travel (–0.18ppts), while fruit & vegetables, utilities
and several other components provide only marginal offsets.

Key details for the month of October
Food & non-alcoholic beverages are expected to rise 0.2% in
October, lifting annual inflation to 3.3%. Strength in the month
is concentrated in meals out & takeaway, where we expect
prices to rise 0.6%mth and 4.3%yr. We have allowed for a
greater-than-usual degree of price resetting as businesses
adjust to the card surcharging ban.
Housing inflation is expected to rise 0.1% in October, with the
annual pace increasing to 6.2% reflecting base effects from

electricity. Across the major components:
• Rents are expected to rise 0.2%mth and 3.5%yr, with the
late-September indexation of the Commonwealth Rent
Assistance flowing through more broadly in October. Our
seasonally adjusted estimate is stronger at 0.4%mth.
• New dwelling inflation has been a key source of downside
surprise in recent months. We expect a 0.2%mth rise
in October, which would see the annual pace moderate
to 4.5%. The downturn in established home prices has

deepened into year-end (see here), which should weigh on
new dwelling inflation over the months ahead. Our current
estimates point to a trough of around 1.3%yr by late-2027.
• Maintenance & repair costs are expected rise a solid
0.9%mth, taking annual inflation to 3.5%, while gas & other
household fuels provide the main offset, falling 2.5%mth.
Transport is expected to be the main source of upward
pressure in October, rising 2.9%mth and lifting to 11.6%yr.

This largely reflects an 8.2%mth jump in auto fuel prices
following the rise in global oil prices through the month. Motor
vehicle prices and spare parts are also expected to increase, by
0.4%mth and 1.6%mth respectively.

Recreation & culture is expected to provide the key offset,

Underlying inflation holds momentum
As noted above, our October TM estimate is also 0.4%mth, a step 
up from 0.3% in September taking the annual pace to 3.7%yr from 
3.6%yr and lifting the six month annualised pace to 4.2%yr, from 
4.0%yr, the fastest pace in the short history of the series. Rents 
and dwellings continue to underpin the TM estimates. 
Upside risks to October baseline ...
We view the risks around the October CPI outcome as slightly 
tilted to the upside. Our estimates already allow for a greater 
degree of price resetting in areas such as meals out & takeaway 
and personal and household services, but there is a risk that this 
adjustment proves broader than we have assumed. Increases in 
fuel levy surcharges in early October are another source of upside 
risk, including increases by Woolworths and Australia Post, with 
the latter nearly doubling its surcharge between September and 
October. The key downside risk remains housing, particularly 
new dwelling prices, which have surprised to the downside in 
recent months and could continue to do so as the housing market 
downturn deepens. Trade notifications data also point to some 
further downside risk, with builders on average announcing 
smaller price changes than in prior months.
... but momentum to ease in November ...
We have a preliminary estimate for the November CPI of 
–0.1%mth which will see the annual pace ease back to 4.6%yr. 
Auto fuel moved lower through November to be down –2.1% in 
the month with seasonal falls in garments, health, furniture & 
household appliances and holiday travel offsetting some strength 
in food, housing, communication and financial services. 
Seasonally adjusted the CPI is estimated to lift 0.1% in November, 
highlighting the seasonal weakness in the month, while our TM 
estimate is for a 0.2% lift seeing the annual pace ease a touch to 
3.6%yr while the six month annualised pace drops from 4.2%yr 
to 3.9%yr. This moderation is expected to continue into December 
with a preliminary TM estimate of 0.2%mth/3.6%yr. 
The following page has a table with monthly profiles for the CPI, 
seasonally adjusted CPI and the TM out to July 2027. By July 
2027 we now expect an annual pace of 3.3%yr for the CPI and 
seasonally adjusted CPI while our estimate for the TM is 3.0%yr. 
More significantly, the six month annualised pace of the TM is 
estimated to be down to 2.7%yr by June 2027, well within the 
RBA’s target band. We have also included tables breaking down 
the monthly components of the CPI through the December and

CPI gains 1.4% in the December quarter highlighting normally

We have for some time expected auto fuel prices to peak in
October, 240 cpl for petrol and 282 cpl for diesel, then to
trend lower from there. Our November estimates are currently
sitting on 237 cpl and 279 cpl respectively trending down to
186 cpl for petrol and 196 cpl for diesel by December 2027.
This represents a 12.7% fall in auto fuel through the first half
of 2027 subtracting 0.42ppt off the CPI over the same period

of time. For a detailed description on how we constructed the
auto fuels profile please see: “Persistent energy pressures
have further to run”. At this stage we have not adjusted our
Brent forecast in line with the current correction underway
(see the November Market Outlook for more details why) as
we expect this to be a volatile ride. Should the current correc-
tion continue, auto fuel will represent a larger drag on the CPI
and potentially the Trimmed Mean.

drop from 5.4%yr at June 2026 to 2.3%yr by June 2027 then

down to 1.3%yr by December 2027.
At least to the end of this year there is still a high degree of
uncertainty about how much businesses will be able to pass
through upstream cost pressures. The latest NAB business sur-
vey continues to highlight robust input price pressure but also

that firms are struggling to pass on those increases.
For businesses, it is almost a now or never opportunity to raise
prices. We expect that through 2027, as energy prices ease
and the domestic economy slows under the weight of higher
interest rates and falling established home prices, households
will be much more resistant to price increases especially if dis-
cretionary household spending softens by more than expected.
That said, these headwinds may be at least partially offset by

the stronger starting point for household incomes, which has
proven more resilient than previously anticipated, and a possi-
ble bounce back in energy prices due to renewed conflict in the
Middle East.
Supply or demand driven inflation?
Inflation has steadily drifted away from the RBA’s target over
recent quarters. While the demand side is the larger driver of
inflation currently, most of the recent pick-up in TM inflation
appears to be from the supply side, and the more detailed

breakdown may understate how much.
There are several ways to split inflation into supply and de-
mand. One is to estimate how much of the rise a standard de-
mand-driven model can explain and attribute the rest to supply
(see here). Run through to Q3 2026, this approach puts most of
the recent pick-up in TM inflation down to supply. Without it,
trimmed mean would have risen from around 3.0%yr to 3.2%yr
over the year to Q3 2026, rather than the 2.9%yr to 3.7%yr

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
rise we actually saw. The supply contribution is moderate,
though, and well below its Covid-era peak. This analysis also
reveals that most of the disinflation through late-2023 and into
early-2025 reflected supply pressures unwinding, while de-
mand-driven inflation remained stickier, persisting above 3%yr.

Another is the San Francisco Fed approach, which the RBA has
also replicated (see here). The attribution is that if prices rise
and quantities fall, it is supply; if both rise, it is demand. We run
it to Q2 2026, as Q3 volume data are not yet available. Like the
first approach, it finds that demand accounts for most of infla-
tion (headline in this case). However, unlike the first approach,
it shows supply contributions easing a little.
A closer look at the categories classified as demand tells a

more nuanced story. Utilities, health and insurance all feature,
and their prices are largely shaped by government policy or are
not the categories that usually come to mind when people talk
about demand-driven inflation, and they are not very sensitive
to interest rates. You can look at electricity as a clear example
where prices in recent history have been set by government
policies (the introduction of, and then removal of, cost of living
rebates as well as policies to decarbonise the economy) or ex-

ternal supply shock (rising gas prices pushing up the marginal
cost of generation).
Categories more commonly thought of discretionary do show
up on the demand side, with recreation & culture (excluding
travel) and meals out & takeaway being the key ones. The
drawback of this sort of decomposition is that it labels cate-
gories where prices and quantities move together as demand,
even when this may reflect the ripple effects of a supply shock
elsewhere, a point our Chief Economist Luci Ellis detailed in a

note in 2024 (see In a world gone bananas, don’t squash the
other fruit). Some of this may be occurring now. In particular,
the analysis is likely not capturing the shift in spending towards
these categories resulting from travel disruptions associated
with the Middle East conflict (travel services itself was classi-
fied as a supply-side contributor in Q2). As travel normalises,
some of the “demand” contribution from recreation & culture
and meals out & takeaway should therefore fade over coming

quarters.
We would also acknowledge the work of Tim Toohey and his
analysis “The RBA has an identity problem”.

Paris, 23 October 2026

EcoFlash

Q3 Growth in advanced economies: solid despite shocks

Growth looks to have held up in Q3 despite successive shocks (see our analysis), as it did in 2025 and H1 2026. A longer 
Middle East conflict has fed into more persistent inflation; drought has worsened in many countries, and interest rates 
have risen further – at the short end, with both the ECB and the Fed raising policy rates in September, and even more at 
the long end. However, our nowcasts show that growth is bending without breaking in most advanced economies. GDP 
figures (due on 29 October in the US and 30 October for the euro area and its member states) are expected to confirm 
that. In the Euro area, momentum looks stable (excluding Ireland, whose GDP is volatile). In Germany, growth has been 
uninterrupted since Q4 2025, while France is likely to have returned to growth after stagnating in H1. In the US, growth 
is likely to have gathered pace, according to the GDP Now, still driven by AI, but increasingly by other sectors as well.

Euro area: resilience beneath the surface. Our nowcast points to growth slowing to 0.3% in q/q, though the pace is stable 
excluding Ireland (Irish GDP is volatile and had markedly boosted the Q2 figure). Excluding Ireland, growth should hold at its H2 
average, a sign of the bloc’s underlying resilience. Also, momentum has broadened to a growing number of sectors (see our 
analysis). While industrial production stalled over the summer, services remained buoyant. The services PMI rebounded sharply 
in Q3 and sits firmly in expansion territory (53.0 in September). Improving job-related survey data (PMI and European Commission 
surveys) and a stable unemployment rate (6.4% in August) hint at more hiring in Q3 – a support for household consumption that 
partly offsets purchasing-power losses from persistent inflation. Corporate investment is underpinned by buoyant new investment 
lending (8.7% y/y In August, 12-month cumulative). 
France: growing, nevertheless. The succession of shocks does not appear to have prevented France from returning to 
growth in Q3, at 0.2% q/q, according to our nowcast. One-off factors accounted for the stall in activity in H1. In Q3, the drought 
worsened and industrial production remained subdued. However, market services production rebounded in July and August, 
notably in business services, restaurants, digital and real estate. The latter points to residential investment recovering after a weak 
H1. Corporate investment likely followed, as reflected in buoyant credit flows and AI-related investment. Household consumption 
is likely to have improved slightly on higher electric vehicle sales.  
Euro area  France

Source: Refinitiv, BNP Paribas  Source: Refinitiv, BNP Paribas

Germany: growth still solid. The H1 rebound in activity is moderating but continues. Our nowcast points to a fourth straight 
quarter of growth in Q3, at 0.2% q/q (after 0.3% in Q4 2025, 0.4% in Q1 2026, and 0.3% in Q2). Manufacturing activity eased 
slightly, according to the September PMI survey (53.9, down 0.4 m/m). Government spending will remain growth-supportive, with 
defence and infrastructure outlays gaining traction. Infrastructure spending continues to underpin activity in public works, which 
stands at its highest since November 2021 (2.5% 3m/3m in August). Exports (4.4% y/y between January and August) remain the 
main growth engine, helped by a pivot towards Europe (7.2%). 
United States: an expansion undeterred by inflation. Growth is likely to have gathered pace in Q3. The Atlanta Fed’s GDP 
Now points to a 3.5% annualized growth rate (AR), close to our own 3.7% forecast (0.9% non-AR) and up from 2.2% in 
Q2. Domestic demand remains the engine. Consumption is weathering the pickup in inflation, while the AI-driven capex cycle 
remains robust – as reflected in retail sales and nondefence capital-goods orders (excl. aircraft). As a result, growth in final sales

### Page 2

to private domestic purchasers1 should print above 4% AR again. By contrast, external trade should remain a drag on growth, 
reflecting AI-related import needs. Survey data point to momentum that is both solid and broad-based. The ISM indices have 
remained firmly in growth territory (54.9 quarterly average for manufacturing and services alike). At the same time, they highlight 
persistent price and supply-chain pressures.

Germany   United States

Source: Refinitiv, BNP Paribas   Source: Refinitiv, Atlanta Fed, BNP Paribas

Document 427: “数学界第一神山”被攻克？Anthropic数学家宣称证明黎曼猜想，给出1700万行Lean源码，AI证明文件超过25万页

Source PDF: all report pdf/“数学界第一神山”被攻克？Anthropic数学家宣称证明黎曼猜想，给出1700万行Lean源码，AI证明文件超过25万页.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-21
Pages: 6
Status: ok

### Page 1

困扰数学界 167 年的黎曼猜想，或被 AI ⾸次攻克。菲尔兹奖得主陶哲轩初步分析认为，证明
融合了多个数学领域的前沿成果，但仍需时间核查。⿇省理⼯学院数学家 Andrew
Sutherland 则称这是历史性时刻。不过，证明尚未通过数学界独⽴验证，⻓达 25 万⻚的⽂
字版证明也让⼈类核查⾯临巨⼤挑战。
摘要：
困扰数学界 167 年的黎曼猜想，或迎来⾸次重⼤突破，但 AI ⽣成的证明能否经受数学界检验，仍
是最⼤悬念。
11 ⽉ 20 ⽇，Anthropic 数学家 Levent Alpöge 发布声明，称⾃⼰与 AI 模型 Claude 合作，找到了
黎曼猜想及⼴义黎曼猜想（ GRH ）的证明⽅法，并同步公布超过 1700 万⾏ Lean 形式化代码。由
于声明发布恰逢 Anthropic 上市次⽇，这项数学突破也引发了外界对发布时间及商业动机的讨
论。
Alpöge 将证明以 Lean 语⾔写成，分为 14 个压缩⽂件发布。与此同时，他与 Claude ⽣成的⽂字版
证明超过 25 万⻚，但因篇幅过⻓、难以阅读⽽没有公开。

⽬前，尚⽆数学家正式确认这⼀证明成⽴。外界仍需核实 Lean 代码最终证明的定理是否确实对
应黎曼猜想本⾝，以及整个证明过程是否存在逻辑漏洞。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

黎曼的经典肖像（ 1850 年代）
证明⽅法： Claude 在 " 终点线 " 实现关键突破
Alpöge 在声明中介绍了此次合作的核⼼过程：他为 Claude 指明了关键研究⽅向，⽽ Claude 随后
提出了他认为极具突破性的关键步骤 —— ⼀条⻓度约等于所界定素数和之平⽅根的 "theta 提
升 " 链。每次提升都对应经典函数域论证所依赖的 " 重数 " ，⽽这⼀重数在整数域上极难找到。
" 在接近终点时， Claude 远远超过了我。不， Claude 简直是在终点附近⻜翔、跳舞、闪
耀， "Alpöge 写道。
他还附上了⼀份据称由 Claude 整理的研究时间线：⼯作始于 10 ⽉ 6 ⽇的 "Stepanov ⾏列式 " ，到 11
⽉ 16 ⽇只剩下⼀条记录 ——"GRH" 。
⿇省理⼯学院数学家 Andrew Sutherland 在独⽴完成相关 Lean 代码编译后表⽰：" 这确实是⼀个
历史性时刻。世界已经改变了 …… 在我看来，这是⼈类的胜利，是值得庆祝的⼀天。 "

数学界反应：谨慎乐观，等待独⽴验证
多位顶尖数学家和 AI 研究⼈员对这⼀进展表达了关注，但对于证明是否真正成⽴，仍保持审慎态
度。
2006 年菲尔兹奖得主陶哲轩在个⼈博客发⽂称，初步分析显⽰，这份证明似乎综合运⽤了朗兰
兹纲领和算术⼏何领域的现代研究成果，验证⼯作预计还需要⼀段时间。
数学家 Elliot Glazer 初步检查发现， Lean ⽂件中形式化的数学命题确实是⼴义黎曼猜想，并估计
Lean ⾃⾝存在漏洞的概率 " 远低于 1%" 。罗格斯⼤学数学系主任 Alex Kontorovich 也表⽰，随着
更多细节浮现，他对证明成功的可能性更有信⼼。
普林斯顿⼤学⼀位数学与 AI 研究⼈员则强调，⼈类参与仍是利⽤⼤语⾔模型解决数学问题的最佳
⽅式。他还指出，在这项研究中， Alpöge 应当获得与 Claude 同等程度的认可。

### Page 3

25 万⻚证明： AI 突破与⼈类验证的⽭盾
证明的庞⼤篇幅，成为数学界关注的另⼀焦点。
曼彻斯特⼤学数学家 Thomas Bloom 在 X 上表⽰，他不知道更令⾃⼰震惊的是 AI 声称证明了黎曼
猜想，还是 AI 声称⽣成了⼀份逻辑连贯、转化为⼈类可读形式后超过 25 万⻚的证明。
Cohere 多模态业务负责⼈、前 Google DeepMind 研究⼈员 Pierre Richemond 则认为，黎曼猜想
可能已经得到解决，但仍有待 Lean 验证。这⼀进展带来了更多问题： AI ⽣成的研究成果已经具
备严肃的数学价值，却尚未经过⼈类数学界的充分验证。

### Page 4

时机争议： IPO 次⽇宣布， Alpöge 主动回应
声明发布时间同样引发外界关注。 Anthropic 于 11 ⽉ 19 ⽇上市，⽽ Alpöge 在次⽇公布这⼀重⼤数
学进展，难免引发市场对其商业宣传动机的猜测。
Alpöge 在声明中主动回应了这⼀争议，承认 " 精确的时机⾮常糟糕 "。他表⽰，关键⼯作于当地
时间 11 ⽉ 15 ⽇深夜⾄ 16 ⽇凌晨完成，随后他向 Anthropic CEO Dario Amodei 询问能否公开， " 最
终我们弄清楚了，确实存在需要遵守的限制 " 。
他否认外界对发布时间的负⾯猜测，称这种 " 明显的愤世嫉俗的看法 " 是错误的，并表⽰⾃⼰ " 实
在⽆法再等下去了 " ，愿意为此承担全部责任。
值得注意的是， Anthropic 此前已在 AI 数学研究⽅⾯取得阶段性进展。今年 8 ⽉，公司公布
Claude 对黎曼 ζ 函数零点分布的研究成果，将关键线上的零点⽐例下界从 41.6% 提升⾄ 67.2% 。
不过，当时 Anthropic 明确表⽰，并不预期 Claude 使⽤的技术能够最终证明黎曼猜想。

奖⾦与认可：距离百万美元奖⾦仍有数年
即便证明最终成⽴， Alpöge 距离克雷数学研究所设⽴的 100 万美元奖⾦仍有相当距离。
按照该机构规则，千禧年⼤奖难题的解答必须经过正式发表、⾄少两年的公开检验，并获得国
际数学界普遍认可，才能申请奖⾦。
Alpöge 本⼈则对证明最终被接受后的形态持乐观态度。他认为，这份证明很可能会被⼤幅简
化，⽽且可能很快就能实现。他将这⼀进展描述为 " 科学领域某种更伟⼤事物的⼀个黎明 " 。

Document 493: 中共５中全会閉幕、次期党大会を見据えて党・軍への統制強化を加速 ～経済政策の実効性と日中関係の行方にも注目～

Source PDF: all report pdf/中共５中全会閉幕、次期党大会を見据えて党・軍への統制強化を加速 ～経済政策の実効性と日中関係の行方にも注目～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 6
Status: ok

### Page 1

Asia Trends

2026 年10 月 30 日

1/6 
中共５中全会閉幕、次期党大会を見据えて党・軍への統制強化を加速 
～経済政策の実効性と日中関係の行方にも注目～ 
 経済調査部 主席エコノミスト  西濵 徹 
（℡：050-5474-7495）

（要旨） 
○ 中国では、10 月26～29 日に中国共産党の第 20 期中央委員会第５回全体会議（５中全会）が
開催された。今回は、従来の慣例とは異なり、「全面的かつ厳格な党内統治」が主要議題と
なり、党規律や幹部の監督、反腐敗の強化、党中央による政策履行の徹底などを盛り込んだ
決定が採択された。また、軍幹部を含む多数の党籍剥奪処分を追認するなど、党・軍に対す
る統制強化の姿勢が鮮明となった。さらに、2027 年後半に第 21 回党大会を開催することが
正式に決定され、習近平指導部は次期党大会に向けて組織・人事面での基盤固めを進める方
針を示したと考えられる。 
○ 一方で、経済政策面では、内需拡大や景気安定に向けた方針が再確認されたものの、具体的
な追加刺激策は示されなかった。足元の中国経済は、外需の堅調さを背景に持ち直しの動き
がみられる一方、不動産不況や消費・投資の低迷など、内需の弱さが依然として課題となっ
ている。こうしたなか、今後は既存の景気支援策の実効性が焦点となるほか、党内統治の厳
格化が行政の政策遂行能力を高めるのか、一方で幹部の責任回避などによる政策執行の遅れ
を招くのかが注目される。 
○ 日本への影響については、中国の内需回復が日本企業の輸出や現地販売を下支えする可能性
がある一方、中国政府による国産化や産業育成の推進が競争圧力を強めることも懸念される。
さらに、重要部材の輸出管理強化や台湾情勢を巡る安全保障上の不確実性が、日本企業の調
達や事業活動に及ぼす影響にも注意が必要となる。今後は、2027 年の次期党大会に向けた組
織・人事の動向に加え、景気支援策の具体的な執行状況、地方行政の対応、対外関係の変化
などを通じて、習近平指導部の政策運営の方向性を見極める必要がある。

【今回の５中全会では、党内統治の強化と政策の履行徹底が主要議題となった】 
中国では、10 月 26 日から 29 日までの日程で中国共産党の第 20 期中央委員会第５回全体会議
（５中全会）が開催された。党大会の閉会期間中に党の指導機関としての役割を担う中央委員会は、
５年の任期中にほぼ１年ごとに１回全体会議を開催し、党の統治や運営上の重要政策、人事などの
議題を討議する。１中全会では新指導部が正式に発足し、２中全会では国務院（政府）などの人事

### Page 2

2/6 
案を承認し、３中全会では経済政策を討議するなど、２中全会と３中全会は通常、党大会の翌年に
開催される。しかし、習近平指導部は３中全会を 2023 年に開催せず、2024 年７月に後ずれした（注
１）。さらに、2025 年 10 月に開始した４中全会では、第 15 次５ヵ年計画（十五五）が討議された
（注２）。 
その結果、通常は５ヵ年計画を討議する場となる５中全会で、今回は「全面的かつ厳格な党内統
治（全面従厳治党）」が主要議題となり、実質的に党大会の前年に開催される６中全会に近い性格を
帯びることとなった。このように、習近平指導部の下では、党の会議の開催時期や議題などについ
て、過去の慣例にとらわれない動きがみられる。 
会議では、習氏が中央政治局を代表して行った活動報告を聴取・討議するとともに、「全面的かつ
厳格な党内統治を持続的に推進するうえでの若干の重大問題に関する決定」を採択した。同決定に
は、党規律、幹部の選抜・監督、組織運営、反腐敗の強化といった内容が盛り込まれた。会議後に
公表された公報によれば、中央の指示を地方や各部門まで徹底するとともに、政治的な忠誠と実務
上の遂行能力を幹部管理に反映させる姿勢がうかがえる。反腐敗を長期的な執政の基盤と位置付け
たうえで、個々の処分にとどまらず、組織的な監督と責任追及の仕組みを整える狙いがあると考え
られる。 
この方向性を考えるうえでは、会議に先立って党の理論誌『求是』に掲載された習氏の演説が重
要な手がかりとなる。これは１月に行われた演説の主要部分を公表したものであり、このなかで習
氏は、党中央の決定が実行されない、または恣意的に実行される問題を挙げるとともに、政治的な
監督の強化の必要性を訴えた。このため、今回の会議においても、政治的忠誠と政策履行の徹底、
反腐敗と監督の制度化、幹部の評価・登用の厳格化が重視されたと考えられる。 
人事面では、中央軍事委員会の前副主席である張又侠氏や元委員の劉振立氏など 33 人に対する
党籍剥奪処分を追認したほか、別の１人に対する党内職務の罷免処分を決定した。党籍剥奪の対象
者は、習近平指導部の下で開催された中央委員会全体会議として過去最多の規模とみられ、うち16
人は元軍関係者が占めた。軍幹部を巡っては、ここ数年の相次ぐ処分が指揮命令系統の弱体化を招
くと懸念されてきた。このため、中央軍事委員会の欠員補充が注目されたものの、今回は同委員会
副主席の張昇民氏を中央政治局委員に追加選出するにとどまった。軍の規律検査を担ってきた張氏
を昇格させることで、次期党大会に向けて軍への統制を強化し、指導体制の立て直しを進める方針
が示されたと考えられる。とはいえ、軍の指揮命令系統を巡る空洞化の問題は残されたままである。
一方、今回は中央委員 24 人が党籍剥奪処分の対象となるなか、中央候補委員 10 人を中央委員に繰
り上げるなど、組織体制の補強も図られた。こうした動きを踏まえれば、今回の５中全会は党・軍
への引き締めを一段と強化する会議となったと捉えられる。

注１ 2024 年７月 19 日付レポート「中国･３中全会､習近平氏が掲げる｢中国式現代化｣の加速を推進」 
  （https://www.dlri.co.jp/report/macro/351237.html） 
注２ 2025 年10 月24 日付レポート「中共４中全会閉幕､中長期的な国力向上の姿勢が鮮明に」 
  （https://www.dlri.co.jp/report/macro/530799.html）

### Page 3

3/6

【2027 年に開催予定の次期党大会に向けて、組織・人事面での基盤整備に注力】 
５中全会では、中国共産党第 21 回全国代表大会（第 21 回党大会）を 2027 年後半に北京で開催
することを正式に決定した。これにより、今後行われる中央・地方の幹部人事や政策運営を評価す
るうえでは、次期党大会に向けた準備という視点がこれまで以上に重要になる。 
５中全会での決定事項を踏まえれば、政治的忠誠と政策遂行能力の重視という観点が、次の指導
部を担う幹部の評価と選抜に関わると考えられる。次期党大会に向けて地方・中央の人事が進めら
れる過程では、党中央の方針をどれだけ着実に実行できるかが問われることになろう。ただし、今
回行われた中央委員の補充は党規約に定められた手続きに沿ったものであり、今回補充された 10
人がそのまま後継指導者候補とはならないことに注意が必要である。 
さらに、思想面から現行指導体制の継続を支える動きも重要であろう。具体的には、公報におい
て、毛沢東思想や鄧小平理論など従来の指導思想に言及するなかで「習近平党建設思想」が明記さ
れ、習近平「新時代の中国の特色ある社会主義」思想の重要な構成要素と位置付けられたことは、
同指導部による党運営の理念を定着させる動きと捉えられる。同思想は、「人民の利益を重視する
（人民至上）」立場を基本としつつ、党統治の厳格化、反腐敗運動の徹底、民主集中制の強化など、
今回の５中全会で討議された内容と重なる要素が多い。なお、この概念は 2026 年６月に開催され
た全国党建設活動座談会で正式に提起・体系化されており、今回初めて登場したものではない。今
後は、次期党大会の政治報告や党規約などにどのような形で反映されるかが注目される。 
前述したように、５中全会では多数の軍幹部が処分されたものの、これは規律による統制を徹底
する姿勢と、人事面での再整備を続ける必要性の双方を示唆している。今回、中央政治局委員に選
出された張升民氏は軍の規律検査を担ってきた経歴を有しており、習近平指導部が人民解放軍に対
する政治的統制を重視してきた姿勢と整合的である。一方で、軍人事の変更だけで軍の実戦能力や
対外行動を直接判断することは難しい。習氏は、台湾統一を目的とする武力行使を選択肢から排除
しておらず、米軍に対抗し得る『世界一流の軍隊』の建設を目指してきた。2027 年８月には人民解
放軍の創設 100 周年を迎えることから、それまでに軍の指揮命令系統の再構築が進めば、同年後半
の党大会で４期目入りを目指すとみられる習氏にとって、権力基盤の一層の安定につながる可能性
がある。

【経済政策面では具体策に乏しいものの、景気安定に向けた政策の実行力が焦点に】 
一方で、経済政策との関係では、十五五の策定過程との区別が必要である。十五五に関する「建
議」は４中全会で採択されるとともに、それを具体化した「綱要」は 2026 年３月の全人代（第 14
期全国人民代表大会第４回全体会議）で承認されている（注３）。このため、今回の５中全会は、こ
うした既存の政策を着実に遂行する組織・幹部の規律を整えるための会議と位置付けられる。

注３ ３月５日付レポート「中国､2026 年全人代開幕､特異性を強める経済､内政､外交」 
  （https://www.dlri.co.jp/report/macro/581059.html）

### Page 4

4/6 
こうしたなか、公報では、マクロ政策の活用と内需拡大を求めるとともに、2026 年の経済・社会
発展目標を達成する方針を再確認した。一方で、追加財政支出の規模や利下げ幅、新たな家計給付
の金額など、具体的な政策措置は示されなかった。今後の政策運営の動きについては、政府や中国
人民銀行（中銀）をはじめとする金融当局が発表する政策の内容に加え、その執行状況などを確認
したうえで評価する必要があると考えられる。 
足元の中国経済を巡っては、経済成長率に持ち直しの動きがみられる一方、内需は力強さを欠く
推移をみせている。７-９月の実質ＧＤＰ成長率は前年同期比＋4.5％と前期（同＋4.3％）から小幅
に加速したほか、前期比年率ベースでは＋4.9％と試算されるなど持ち直しの動きが確認されてい
る（注４）。 一方で、９月までの累計ベースの小売売上高（社会消費品小売総額）は前年比＋1.1％
にとどまり、固定資産投資は同▲7.2％とマイナスで推移するとともに、なかでも不動産投資は同
▲20.6％と大幅に下振れしている。こうしたなか、足元の景気は外需の旺盛な動きを追い風にした
ハイテク製造業を中心とする供給サイドがけん引役となる展開が続いており、国内の需給不均衡が
解消されない状況にある。国家統計局も、内需の弱さを経済運営上の課題として認識しており、景
気の回復力にはなお弱さが残ると考えられる。 
このため、金融市場では、2027 年後半に開催される党大会を前に、景気や雇用、社会の安定を維
持する観点から、何らかの政策対応が強化されるとの見方が根強い。とはいえ、内需の低迷が長期
化しているにもかかわらず、これまで大規模な景気刺激策に積極姿勢をみせてこなかったことを勘
案すれば、政治日程のみを材料に積極姿勢に転じると見込むことは難しい。国務院は７月、同国で
初めて個人消費に焦点を当てた「消費拡大の５ヵ年計画」を公表した。同計画では、2030 年の小売
売上高（社会消費品小売総額）の規模を 2025 年（50.1 兆元）に比べて約２割（60 兆元前後）引き
上げるべく、消費構造の高度化や家計所得の引き上げを目指すとした。加えて、共産党は７月末に
開催した中央政治局会議において、インフラ関連など財政支出の進捗加速による景気下支えを図る
方針を示した。これを受けて、政府・金融当局は８月以降、内需促進を目的とする新たな財政・金
融支援策を発表しており、今後はこれらの政策がどの程度の効果を上げるかを見定める必要がある。 
一方で、今後焦点となり得るのが、党内統治の厳格化と政策実行力の関係であろう。汚職や形式
的な行政対応が減るとともに、党中央・政府が提示した内需支援策が適切に執行されれば、政策の
実効性が高まることが期待される。ただし、幹部が責任追及を警戒して判断を先送りするほか、上
位機関への照会を増やすような動きが広がれば、企業の許認可や政策資金の執行が遅れる可能性は
残る。今回の決定のみをもって政策効果の行方を判断することはできず、地方レベルでの行政実務
の動向を注視する必要があろう。その意味では、政策の規模に加え、資金の使途と執行状況を点検
することが重要になる。仮に、今後も供給サイドの改革が優先されるとともに、産業設備投資への
支援が集中すれば、需要に見合わない生産能力の増強を招くとともに、国内における価格競争や対
外摩擦を強める可能性に注意が必要となる。

注４ 10 月19 日付レポート「中国・７-９月 GDP は前年比＋4.5％に加速も、外需依存の構図は変わらず」 
  （https://www.dlri.co.jp/report/macro/666711.html）

### Page 5

5/6

【日本への影響を巡っては、中国の内需動向、行政運営、産業競争、供給網などの動向に注意】 
日本にとって中国は、需要先であると同時に重要な供給元でもある。2025 年時点において、日本
の貿易に占める中国の割合は、輸出で 17.0％、輸入で 23.6％となっている。中国の需要動向は日
本の輸出や現地販売に、供給面の変化は日本の調達や生産活動に影響を及ぼす。このため、５中全
会で示された党内統治の強化の行方が、今後どのような形で政策運営や行政実務に反映されるかが
重要になる。 
まず、内需の行方は、中国に進出する日系企業の販売機会に直結すると予想される。当局が主導
する家計所得や雇用への支援が実効性を持つことができれば、消費関連の日系企業の現地販売のほ
か、日本からの輸出を支えることが期待される。一方で、産業設備への投資が中心となる場合には、
機械・電子部品・素材などに需要が向かいやすい。ただし、支援対象、現地調達条件、日本企業の
競争力によって恩恵は異なると予想されるほか、中国の景気対策の規模がそのまま日本の輸出増に
つながるとは限らない点には注意が必要である。 
次に、行政運営が事業環境に及ぼす影響にも注意が必要となる。規律強化が恣意的な行政や不透
明な取引を減らせば、企業の事業環境に対する予見性を高める効果が期待できる。反対に、担当者
の交代や責任追及への警戒が判断の遅れを招く場合には、事業認可、政府調達、契約履行などに支
障が生じ得る。中国日本商会が発表した 2026 年白書においても、行政手続の迅速化、制度運用の
統一、内外企業の公平な競争条件の確保などが求められている。日本企業にとっては、こうした改
善が実務レベルでどの程度進展するかが重要な判断材料になる。 
さらに、中国国内における産業育成に伴う需要と競争環境の行方にも注意が必要となる。習近平
指導部が掲げる科学技術の自立自強やサプライチェーンの強靱化、「新質生産力」の育成といった
方針は、すでに十五五の策定段階で重視されている。今後、こうした動きが一段と強力に推進され
れば、日本の設備・部材メーカーに販売機会をもたらすことが期待される一方、国産化が進んだ製
品については中期的に競争圧力が強まる可能性がある。すでに、太陽光電池や風力発電設備につい
ては、中国メーカーが世界市場で高いシェアを有するほか、ＥＶ（電気自動車）でも高い国際競争
力を持つ。中国市場における需要の行方だけでなく、日本企業の技術や製品が代替される可能性の
ほか、第三国市場での競争激化が一段と加速する可能性にも注意が必要である。さらに、世界的に
ＡＩ（人工知能）・半導体を巡る開発競争が激化するなか、７月には中国が主導して世界ＡＩ機構
（WAICO）という国際機関を設立するなど、この分野をリードする姿勢を強めており、その動向にも
注意が必要である。 
足元で特に注視すべきなのが、重要部材の供給と安全保障を巡る影響であろう。このところの中
国は、高市首相による台湾有事を巡る国会答弁や日本の防衛政策に対する反発を強めるなか、日本
に対する「経済的威圧」を強めている。中国商務部は１月､日本に対する軍民両用品（デュアルユー
ス）の輸出管理を強化する方針を発表し、その後も対象を拡大するなど、規制を強化してきた。足
元では、レアアース関連の輸出許可や通関手続きの運用を巡る不確実性が、企業実務上の重要な課
題となっている。今後も、対象品目や審査基準に関する当局の個別公告に加え、企業による輸出許

### Page 6

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
6/6 
可の取得や調達の動向を確認する必要があることは間違いない。 
安全保障面についても、軍幹部の処分や政治局人事をもって、台湾有事の時期や可能性を判断す
ることは困難である。日本への影響については、台湾海峡や東シナ海における実際の軍事活動、日
中間の外交対話、経済措置の変化を通じて判断する必要がある。企業にとっては、物流、重要部材
の調達、現地拠点の運営にどのような支障が生じ得るかを、平時から具体的に点検することの重要
性がこれまで以上に高まっていると判断できる。

【今後は、政策の実施内容と現場レベルでの反応を確認する必要性が高まっている】 
先行きについては、現行路線の継続と党大会に向けた景気安定重視が基本線となる一方、行政の
実行力や対外関係の行方によって実際の展開が左右されると考えられる。とりわけ、幹部人事と次
期指導部の形成過程、家計支援・不動産対策・産業支援の具体的内容と執行状況、日系企業の許認
可・代金回収・調達の実態、輸出管理や外交・安全保障上の具体的な変化を継続的に確認する必要
性は高まっている。次期党大会で示される政治報告と、その後に開催される１中全会での指導部人
事の行方は、その先の政策運営を評価する重要な材料となる。その意味では、開催まで約１年とな
った次期党大会に向けて、党中央と地方組織双方の動きをこれまで以上に注視する必要があると考
えられる。

### Page 1

（備考）2025年度までの実績は内閣府「国民経済計算」により作成。

１. 世界の実質ＧＤＰ成長率は、2022 年以降３％台半ばで推移しているが、少子高齢化を背
景とした新興国の成長鈍化により、予測期間の終期の 2036 年には２％台後半まで低下す
ると予想する。 
２. 日本の実質ＧＤＰ成長率は、2036 年度までの先行き 10 年間の平均で 0.9％と予想する。
潜在成長率は足下のゼロ％台後半から 2020 年代後半に１％程度まで高まった後、少子高
齢化の更なる進展に伴い 2030年代前半にゼロ％台後半まで鈍化すると見込む。 
３. 消費者物価上昇率（生鮮食品を除く総合）は、先行き 10 年間の平均で 2.1％と予想する。
企業等の各経済主体の期待物価上昇率は２％前後となってきている。中東情勢の悪化に
よる物価上昇が落ち着くにつれ、2028 年度以降は、飲食料品の消費税率の引下げ・引上
げの影響を除けば２％程度の安定的な物価上昇が実現すると見込む。 
４. 日本銀行は、当面は物価上昇が再燃する中、消費者物価上昇率を２％程度に安定させてい
くため、段階的に利上げを実施し、政策金利は 2027 年度に1.75％まで引き上げられると
見込む。長期金利は、長期国債の買い入れ減額の継続に加え、国内の貯蓄超過の縮小が上
昇圧力になることから、2036 年度には３％台後半まで上昇すると予想する。 
ニッセイ基礎研究所 2026-10-13

中期経済見通し（2026～2036 年度）

経済研究部

### Page 2

主要先進国の成長率はコロナ禍で大きく落ち込んだものの、その後は相次ぐショックにもかかわ
らず底堅く推移している。ホルムズ海峡の輸送が制約される中では、世界的な原油在庫の取り崩し、
エネルギー需要抑制や政府主導での節約措置、供給経路・供給源の多様化（中東産原油の代替ルー
トでの輸送や中東以外の原油増産）などが供給制約ショックを吸収している。 
インフレ率は 2022 年のエネルギー価格高騰などを背景に急上昇したのち、2025 年にかけて緩や
かに低下してきた。しかし、コロナ禍前の水準まで低下しないまま、中東情勢の悪化で再び上昇圧
力が強まっている。2022年のインフレ加速期に、主要中央銀行による金融引き締めが遅れた経験も
あり、中央銀行はインフレ警戒感を強めており、利上げに転じる国も増えつつある。

（ＡＩ関連需要が成長率を押し上げ） 
成長率の底堅さの要因として、足もとで顕著となっているのが世界的なＡＩ関連需要の高まりで
ある。半導体など電子部品需要、データセンターを中心とした建設需要、電力などのインフラ投資
需要が増加している。世界半導体売上高（3か月移動平均）は急増しており、2026 年5 月以降、前
年比で 2倍を超えている。米中を中心にＡＩ開発が急速に進んでいるほか、ＡＩ関連需要は貿易を
通じて特にアジアの生産・輸出を押し上げるなど、世界に波及している。

世界 金融政策動向（ＤＩ）
（月次）

### Page 3

こうした状況下、主要先進国では長期金利の上昇圧力が強まっている。インフレ圧力の強まりと
中央銀行の利上げ観測のほか、政府の財政支出拡大懸念や民間部門での資金調達需要が金利の押し
上げ圧力となっている。 
財政支出に関して、米国では主要財源と見られた関税引き上げ政策のうち、最高裁判所により国
際緊急経済権限法（ＩＥＥＰＡ）に基づく関税が無効と判断され、歳入の不透明感が増した。欧州
ではトランプ政権による安全保障政策の方針転換を背景に防衛費を拡大する動きが進む。日本では、
ガソリン価格抑制策など物価高対策が長期化するなか、「責任ある積極財政」を掲げる高市政権の
財政支出に注目が集まっている。民間部門の資金調達需要の高まりは、巨大ＩＴ企業によるＡＩ関
連投資の拡大などが背景にある。

（経済を取り巻く不確実性は引き続き高い） 
世界経済を取り巻く不確実性は高い状態が続いている。トランプ大統領の講じる政策は予見可能
性が低い。今後も通商領域やその他の領域で国家間の摩擦が強まる可能性がある。短期的な経済シ
ョックが生じうるだけでなく、中長期的にはトランプ大統領の米国第一主義や既存の国際秩序や多
国間枠組みを見直す政策姿勢がドル資産への需要やドルの基軸通貨としての地位に影響を及ぼす
可能性もあるだろう。地政学的リスクも大きく、すでにウクライナや中東地域では地政学的要因が
経済に悪影響を及ぼしている。原油在庫の取り崩しや供給源の多様化といったバッファーには量的

### Page 4

（世界の実質経済成長率は緩やかに低下） 
世界経済は、新型コロナウイルス感染症の影響で 2020 年に▲2.7％の大幅なマイナス成長となっ
た後、2021年はその反動で 6.7％の高成長となった。その後は、ロシアによるウクライナ侵攻、米
国による関税率の大幅な引き上げ、中東情勢の悪化など、相次ぐショックに見舞われてきた。しか
し、世界経済はこうした逆風のなかでも底堅さを保ち、実質経済成長率は 2022年以降、3％台半ば
で推移している。中期的には予測期間にわたって緩やかな鈍化傾向をたどり、予測期間末の2036 年
には 2％台後半まで低下することが見込まれる。 
先行きの成長率を先進国と新興国に分けてみると、新興国の成長率は先進国を一貫して上回ると
みられる。しかし、少子高齢化に伴い潜在成長率の低下が進むことなどを背景に、新興国の成長率
寄与度は予測期間後半に 2％台前半まで低下すると予想する。

世界経済に占める新興国の割合（ドルベース）は、2000 年の 20％程度から足もとでは 40％程度
まで上昇している。新興国の成長率は今後緩やかに低下するものの、先進国を上回る成長が続くこ
とから、その割合は予測期間末には 40％台半ばまで高まるだろう。

### Page 5

国別にみると、世界第 2 位の経済規模を持つ中国は、2018 年に経済規模でユーロ圏を上回るな
ど、コロナ禍前まで世界経済に占める割合を拡大させてきた。しかし、先行きについては、中国の
名目成長率が予測期間後半にかけて緩やかに鈍化し、人民元の対ドル相場が横ばい圏にとどまるこ
とから、予測期間中は米国の経済規模が中国を一貫して上回ると見込まれる。 
中国に匹敵する人口を抱えるインドは、予測期間中は人口増加が続くことから高い潜在成長率を
期待でき、世界経済に占める割合を高めていくとみられる。2020 年代後半にはインドの経済規模が
日本を上回ることが予想される。 
一人当たりＧＤＰ（ドルベース）をみると、日本は 1980年代後半から 1990 年代にかけて米国を
上回っていたが、2000 年頃にその関係が逆転した後は一貫して米国を下回っている。日本の一人当
たりＧＤＰは円安の影響もあり、2025 年には米国の 4 割程度の水準まで低下した。今後 10 年間の
日本の平均成長率は米国を下回ることが予想されるため、予測期間終盤も日本は米国の 4割弱の水
準で推移することとなるだろう。また、ユーロ圏と比較すると、2025年時点で日本の一人当たりＧ
ＤＰはおよそ 7 割であるが、予測期間の平均成長率がユーロ圏を下回るため、予測期間後半には 6
割程度まで低下するだろう。 
一方、日本のＧＤＰの水準は 2010年に中国に抜かれたが、一人当たりＧＤＰでみれば 2025 年時
点でも中国の2.5 倍程度の水準にある。今後は中国の成長率が日本を大きく上回るが、予測期間末
でも日本の一人当たりＧＤＰは中国の 2倍程度の水準を維持するだろう。また、予測期間中盤に日
本のＧＤＰを抜くインドは、一人当たりＧＤＰでみれば 2025 年時点では日本の約 7％となってい
るが、10年後には 11％程度の水準まで緩やかに上昇するだろう。
